EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

min_{lag=-3,...,3} ||x_overlap-y_overlap||_1

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Shifted Manhattan evaluates sum of absolute overlap errors after each permitted integer shift, using only samples that overlap. This example admits lags from minus three to plus three samples, leaving at least seven paired observations. The animation shows the actual overlap pairs and candidate scores before selecting their minimum.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

min_{lag=-3,...,3} mean(abs(x_overlap-y_overlap))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Shifted MAE evaluates mean absolute overlap error after each permitted integer shift, using only samples that overlap. This example admits lags from minus three to plus three samples, leaving at least seven paired observations. The animation shows the actual overlap pairs and candidate scores before selecting their minimum.

How the calculation works

  2. At each lag, compute mean absolute overlap error on the actual paired samples.
  3. Record every candidate score together with its lag.

Interpretation and limits

  • The bounded window is an explicit demonstration choice; allowing every possible lag changes the problem.
  • Overlap cropping removes unmatched endpoints from this score.

Shifted cosine similarity shift_cosine_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative similarity: 0.99992749 —

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

max_{lag=-3,...,3} cosine(x_overlap,y_overlap)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Shifted cosine similarity evaluates cosine on the current overlap after each permitted integer shift, using only samples that overlap. This example admits lags from minus three to plus three samples, leaving at least seven paired observations. The animation shows the actual overlap pairs and candidate scores before selecting their maximum.

How the calculation works

  1. Fix the permitted lags and minimum acceptable overlap before scoring.
  2. At each lag, compute cosine on the current overlap on the actual paired samples.
  3. Record every candidate score together with its lag.
  4. Choose the maximum overlap score.

Interpretation and limits

  • Every overlap has its own norm; this is different from full-norm NCC.
  • The bounded window is an explicit demonstration choice; allowing every possible lag changes the problem.
  • Overlap cropping removes unmatched endpoints from this score.

  2. 对每个滞后的实际配对样本计算对应分数。
  3. 记录所有候选分数及其滞后。
  4. 选取最大重叠分数。

Shifted cosine distance shift_cosine_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 7.251435e-05 —

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

1 - max_{lag=-3,...,3} cosine(x_overlap,y_overlap)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Shifted cosine distance evaluates cosine on the current overlap after each permitted integer shift, using only samples that overlap. This example admits lags from minus three to plus three samples, leaving at least seven paired observations. The animation shows the actual overlap pairs and candidate scores before selecting their maximum. The final one-minus transform produces the displayed dissimilarity.

How the calculation works

  1. Fix the permitted lags and minimum acceptable overlap before scoring.
  2. At each lag, compute cosine on the current overlap on the actual paired samples.
  3. Record every candidate score together with its lag.
  4. Choose the maximum and apply 1-score.

Interpretation and limits

  • The one-minus transform does not establish a metric on raw time series.
  • The bounded window is an explicit demonstration choice; allowing every possible lag changes the problem.
  • Overlap cropping removes unmatched endpoints from this score.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

Shifted cosine distance 在每个允许的整数平移后，仅对重叠样本计算对应分数。本例滞后范围为 −3 至 +3 个采样点，始终保留至少七对观测。动图展示实际配对和候选分数，再选择最大值。最后取 1 减分数得到差异量。

计算步骤

  1. 先固定允许滞后与最小可接受重叠长度。
  2. 对每个滞后的实际配对样本计算对应分数。
  3. 记录所有候选分数及其滞后。
  4. 选择最大分数后取 1 减。

解释与使用边界

  • 1 减变换不能自动证明原时间序列上的度量性质。
  • 有界窗口是明确的演示选择；允许所有滞后会改变问题。
  • 重叠裁剪会从分数中排除未匹配的两端。

    -3,
    -2,
    -1,
    0,
    1,
    2,
    3
  ],
  "minimum_overlap": 7,
  "normalization": "per overlap",
  "selected_lag": -2
}

Shifted Pearson correlation shift_pearson_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative similarity: 0.9998975 —

EchoTime coverage: Conceptually covered · best_lag_pearson_r

max_{lag=-3,...,3} corr(x_overlap,y_overlap)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Shifted Pearson correlation evaluates Pearson r on the current overlap after each permitted integer shift, using only samples that overlap. This example admits lags from minus three to plus three samples, leaving at least seven paired observations. The animation shows the actual overlap pairs and candidate scores before selecting their maximum.

How the calculation works

  1. Fix the permitted lags and minimum acceptable overlap before scoring.
  2. At each lag, compute Pearson r on the current overlap on the actual paired samples.
  3. Record every candidate score together with its lag.
  4. Choose the maximum overlap score.

Interpretation and limits

  • Each overlap is independently centered; selecting the best lag also selects among several correlations.
  • The bounded window is an explicit demonstration choice; allowing every possible lag changes the problem.
  • Overlap cropping removes unmatched endpoints from this score.

计算步骤

  1. 先固定允许滞后与最小可接受重叠长度。
  2. 对每个滞后的实际配对样本计算对应分数。
  3. 记录所有候选分数及其滞后。
  4. 选取最大重叠分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 每个重叠片段分别去均值；选取最佳滞后同时也是在多个相关结果中选择。
  • 有界窗口是明确的演示选择；允许所有滞后会改变问题。
  • 重叠裁剪会从分数中排除未匹配的两端。

Shifted Pearson distance shift_pearson_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.00010249589 —

EchoTime coverage: Conceptually covered · best_lag_pearson_r

1 - max_{lag=-3,...,3} corr(x_overlap,y_overlap)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Shifted Pearson distance evaluates Pearson r on the current overlap after each permitted integer shift, using only samples that overlap. This example admits lags from minus three to plus three samples, leaving at least seven paired observations. The animation shows the actual overlap pairs and candidate scores before selecting their maximum. The final one-minus transform produces the displayed dissimilarity.

How the calculation works

  1. Fix the permitted lags and minimum acceptable overlap before scoring.
  2. At each lag, compute Pearson r on the current overlap on the actual paired samples.
  3. Record every candidate score together with its lag.
  4. Choose the maximum and apply 1-score.

Interpretation and limits

  • One minus the selected correlation is an explicit dissimilarity, not a probability or significance test.
  • The bounded window is an explicit demonstration choice; allowing every possible lag changes the problem.
  • Overlap cropping removes unmatched endpoints from this score.

  1. 先固定允许滞后与最小可接受重叠长度。
  2. 对每个滞后的实际配对样本计算对应分数。
  3. 记录所有候选分数及其滞后。
  4. 选择最大分数后取 1 减。

  • 有界窗口是明确的演示选择；允许所有滞后会改变问题。

Independent channel SBD independent_sbdChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.068217211 —

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · independent_sbd

mean_c (1-max_lag NCC_c(lag))

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

Independent channel comparison permits each paired channel to choose a different global lag. The illustration uses two channels with peaks shifted in opposite directions, then averages their individually optimized scores. For SBD it averages one minus each channel's maximum NCC. The upper input plot shows channel one; both channels are displayed in the computational frames.

How the calculation works

  1. Match channels explicitly and normalize each series within each channel.
  2. Compute the full-norm NCC lag profile for channel one.
  3. Repeat the lag scan independently for channel two.
  4. Average channel-wise dissimilarities.

Interpretation and limits

  • Independent lags discard cross-channel timing consistency; choose a joint-lag method if synchrony matters.
  • Channels are averaged equally here; this is not a learned weighting.
  • The channel pairing must have a scientific meaning.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

独立通道比较允许每对通道选择不同的全局滞后。本例使用两组峰值向相反方向偏移的通道，再平均各自优化后的分数。SBD 型平均每个通道的 1 减最大 NCC。上方输入图显示第一通道，计算帧依次展示两个通道。

计算步骤

  1. 明确通道配对，并在每个通道内分别标准化。
  2. 计算第一通道的全范数 NCC 滞后曲线。
  3. 独立计算第二通道的滞后曲线。
  4. 平均通道差异量。

解释与使用边界

  • 独立滞后舍弃跨通道时序一致性；同步关系重要时应使用共同滞后的方法。
  • 本例对通道等权平均，不学习权重。
  • 通道配对必须有科学意义。

Independent channel maximum NCC independent_max_nccChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative similarity: 0.93178279 —

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · independent_max_ncc

mean_c max_lag NCC_c(lag)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

Independent channel comparison permits each paired channel to choose a different global lag. The illustration uses two channels with peaks shifted in opposite directions, then averages their individually optimized scores. For independent maximum NCC it averages the channel maxima. The upper input plot shows channel one; both channels are displayed in the computational frames.

How the calculation works

  1. Match channels explicitly and normalize each series within each channel.
  2. Compute the full-norm NCC lag profile for channel one.
  3. Repeat the lag scan independently for channel two.
  4. Average the maxima; no common lag is required.

Interpretation and limits

  • Independent lags discard cross-channel timing consistency; choose a joint-lag method if synchrony matters.
  • Channels are averaged equally here; this is not a learned weighting.
  • The channel pairing must have a scientific meaning.

  • 本例对通道等权平均，不学习权重。

Elastic alignment and edit operations / 弹性对齐与编辑操作 24
Dynamic time warping dtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.4472136 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Conceptually covered · dtw_similarity

DTW(x,y) = sqrt(min_path sum_(i,j) (x_i-y_j)^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

DTW compares ordered series while allowing repeated matches along a monotone alignment path. The animation builds squared pointwise costs, fills the dynamic-programming table, and traces a minimum-cost path. This reference example returns the square root of the total cost; EchoTime's composite dtw_similarity uses a different local cost and scaling.

How the calculation works

  1. Choose raw observations and any neighborhood descriptor.
  2. Compute local squared discrepancies; apply the indicated weights or admissibility mask.
  3. At each admissible cell, add its local cost to the smallest predecessor cost.
  4. Trace one minimizing path and take the square root of its accumulated objective.

Interpretation and limits

  • Unrestricted warping can align events that should remain distinct; the output is not generally a metric.
  • Signal scale, path constraints and final normalization must match before distances from different tools are compared.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

DTW 在保持先后顺序的前提下，允许同一个观测与多个观测匹配。动图依次展示平方局部误差、动态规划累计表和最低代价路径。本例返回累计代价的平方根；EchoTime 的复合 dtw_similarity 使用不同的局部代价与缩放。

计算步骤

  1. 选择原始观测以及需要的邻域描述符。
  2. 计算局部平方误差，并施加指定权重或允许匹配的掩码。
  3. 在每个允许单元格，把局部代价加到三个前驱的最小累计代价上。
  4. 回溯一条最小化目标的路径，并对其累计目标值取平方根。

解释与使用边界

  • 无限制扭曲可能对齐本应区分的事件；该输出一般不满足全部度量公理。
  • 比较不同工具的数值之前，须统一信号尺度、路径约束和最终归一化。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "final_square_root": true,
  "normalization": "none"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: DTW definitions and path constraints; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

DTW with a path band cdtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.4472136 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Conceptually covered · dtw_similarity

cDTW = sqrt(min_path sum (x_i-y_j)^2), with |i-j| <= 1

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Constrained DTW restricts the allowed correspondences before optimizing their cumulative cost. Here only cells at most one sample from the diagonal are admissible. Empty cells in the animation are forbidden states, not zero-cost matches; the same recurrence operates on the remaining band.

How the calculation works

  1. Choose raw observations and any neighborhood descriptor.
  2. Compute local squared discrepancies; apply the indicated weights or admissibility mask.
  3. At each admissible cell, add its local cost to the smallest predecessor cost.
  4. Trace one minimizing path and take the square root of its accumulated objective.

Interpretation and limits

  • A narrow band can exclude the desired alignment. Unequal-length inputs require an endpoint-aware constraint.
  • Signal scale, path constraints and final normalization must match before distances from different tools are compared.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

约束 DTW 先限制允许匹配的位置，再优化累计误差。本例只允许偏离对角线不超过一个采样点的单元格。动图中的空格表示禁止状态，不能当作零代价；其余带状区域采用相同的递推。

计算步骤

  1. 选择原始观测以及需要的邻域描述符。
  2. 计算局部平方误差，并施加指定权重或允许匹配的掩码。
  3. 在每个允许单元格，把局部代价加到三个前驱的最小累计代价上。
  4. 回溯一条最小化目标的路径，并对其累计目标值取平方根。

解释与使用边界

  • 窄窗口可能排除希望找到的匹配；长度不同时还须确保约束允许连接两端。
  • 比较不同工具的数值之前，须统一信号尺度、路径约束和最终归一化。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "final_square_root": true,
  "normalization": "none",
  "window": 1
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: DTW definitions and path constraints; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

DTW with slope constraints itakura_dtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.74161985 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

Allowed: i/2 <= j <= 2i and (n-1-i)/2 <= n-1-j <= 2(n-1-i)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

An Itakura-style parallelogram limits how strongly an alignment can stretch near either endpoint. This equal-length illustration uses slopes one half and two, anchored at the first and last samples. Dynamic programming then finds the least-cost path inside that explicitly shown region.

How the calculation works

  1. Choose raw observations and any neighborhood descriptor.
  2. Compute local squared discrepancies; apply the indicated weights or admissibility mask.
  3. At each admissible cell, add its local cost to the smallest predecessor cost.
  4. Trace one minimizing path and take the square root of its accumulated objective.

Interpretation and limits

  • Mask discretization and unequal-length slope conventions differ across libraries; this card specifies an equal-length integer-grid example.
  • Signal scale, path constraints and final normalization must match before distances from different tools are compared.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

Itakura 型平行四边形限制对齐路径在两端附近的伸缩程度。本例长度相等，允许斜率范围为二分之一至二，并同时锚定首尾观测。动态规划在图示允许区域内寻找最低代价路径。

计算步骤

  1. 选择原始观测以及需要的邻域描述符。
  2. 计算局部平方误差，并施加指定权重或允许匹配的掩码。
  3. 在每个允许单元格，把局部代价加到三个前驱的最小累计代价上。
  4. 回溯一条最小化目标的路径，并对其累计目标值取平方根。

解释与使用边界

  • 不同库的离散掩码和异长斜率约定可能不同；本卡明确采用等长整数网格示例。
  • 比较不同工具的数值之前，须统一信号尺度、路径约束和最终归一化。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "final_square_root": true,
  "normalization": "none",
  "itakura": true
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: DTW definitions and path constraints; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Weighted dynamic time warping wdtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.12037329 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

w(k)=1/(1+exp(-0.6*(k-L/2))); d=sqrt(min_path sum w(|i-j|)*(x_i-y_j)^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Weighted DTW changes the price of a match according to its displacement from the diagonal. The local squared discrepancy is multiplied by a logistic weight that increases with displacement. The animation then optimizes those weighted costs and displays the resulting path, without imposing a hard band.

How the calculation works

  1. Choose raw observations and any neighborhood descriptor.
  2. Compute local squared discrepancies; apply the indicated weights or admissibility mask.
  3. At each admissible cell, add its local cost to the smallest predecessor cost.
  4. Trace one minimizing path and take the square root of its accumulated objective.

Interpretation and limits

  • These weights are at most one: the penalty is relative across displacements, not an added nonnegative surcharge to ordinary DTW.
  • Signal scale, path constraints and final normalization must match before distances from different tools are compared.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

加权 DTW 根据匹配位置偏离对角线的程度改变代价。局部平方误差乘以随位移增加的逻辑函数权重，再对这些加权代价求最优路径。本例不施加硬窗口。

计算步骤

  1. 选择原始观测以及需要的邻域描述符。
  2. 计算局部平方误差，并施加指定权重或允许匹配的掩码。
  3. 在每个允许单元格，把局部代价加到三个前驱的最小累计代价上。
  4. 回溯一条最小化目标的路径，并对其累计目标值取平方根。

解释与使用边界

  • 这里的权重不超过一；所谓惩罚表示不同位移之间的相对权重，不是给普通 DTW 额外增加非负费用。
  • 比较不同工具的数值之前，须统一信号尺度、路径约束和最终归一化。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "final_square_root": true,
  "normalization": "none",
  "weighted": true,
  "g": 0.6
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: aeon: logistic weighted DTW; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

DTW on first differences ddtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.89442719 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Conceptually covered · dtw_similarity

Delta x_i=x_(i+1)-x_i; d=DTW(Delta x,Delta y), squared costs then square root

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

This atlas entry applies DTW to first differences, so the alignment responds to changes rather than absolute levels. The first frames expose the transformed series before the local table is accumulated. It is an explicit first-difference variant; other DDTW implementations use a smoothed derivative estimator.

How the calculation works

  1. Take first differences with the stated convention.
  2. Compute local squared discrepancies; apply the indicated weights or admissibility mask.
  3. At each admissible cell, add its local cost to the smallest predecessor cost.
  4. Trace one minimizing path and take the square root of its accumulated objective.

Interpretation and limits

  • Differencing removes constant offsets and amplifies high-frequency noise. The result depends on the derivative estimator.
  • Signal scale, path constraints and final normalization must match before distances from different tools are compared.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

本条目对一阶差分使用 DTW，因此匹配依据是变化而非绝对水平。动图先展示变换后的序列，再构造累计表。本例明确使用一阶差分；其他 DDTW 实现可能采用平滑导数估计。

计算步骤

  1. 按明确约定计算一阶差分。
  2. 计算局部平方误差，并施加指定权重或允许匹配的掩码。
  3. 在每个允许单元格，把局部代价加到三个前驱的最小累计代价上。
  4. 回溯一条最小化目标的路径，并对其累计目标值取平方根。

解释与使用边界

  • 差分消除常数偏移，也会放大高频噪声；结果取决于导数估计方法。
  • 比较不同工具的数值之前，须统一信号尺度、路径约束和最终归一化。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "final_square_root": true,
  "normalization": "none",
  "derivative": "adjacent first differences; n-1 outputs"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: DTW definitions and path constraints; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Weighted DTW on differences wddtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.23646036 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d=sqrt(min_path sum w(|i-j|)*(Delta x_i-Delta y_j)^2), g=0.6

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

This variant first takes adjacent differences and then uses displacement-weighted DTW. It combines sensitivity to local changes with a preference for smaller index displacement. The displayed derivative table and weighted path belong to that stated composition, which need not equal every library's WDDTW convention.

How the calculation works

  1. Take first differences with the stated convention.
  2. Compute local squared discrepancies; apply the indicated weights or admissibility mask.
  3. At each admissible cell, add its local cost to the smallest predecessor cost.
  4. Trace one minimizing path and take the square root of its accumulated objective.

Interpretation and limits

  • Both derivative estimation and the logistic slope change the answer; offset tolerance does not imply robustness to arbitrary noise.
  • Signal scale, path constraints and final normalization must match before distances from different tools are compared.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

该变体先计算相邻差分，再进行位移加权 DTW，结合局部变化比较与较小索引位移偏好。图中导数表和加权路径对应这里明确给出的组合，不保证与所有库的 WDDTW 约定相同。

计算步骤

  1. 按明确约定计算一阶差分。
  2. 计算局部平方误差，并施加指定权重或允许匹配的掩码。
  3. 在每个允许单元格，把局部代价加到三个前驱的最小累计代价上。
  4. 回溯一条最小化目标的路径，并对其累计目标值取平方根。

解释与使用边界

  • 导数估计和逻辑权重斜率都会改变结果；容忍水平偏移并不意味着对任意噪声稳健。
  • 比较不同工具的数值之前，须统一信号尺度、路径约束和最终归一化。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "final_square_root": true,
  "normalization": "none",
  "derivative": "adjacent first differences; n-1 outputs",
  "weighted": true,
  "g": 0.6
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: aeon: logistic weighted DTW; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Soft dynamic time warping soft_dtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative soft objective: -4.4439633 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

R_ij=(x_i-y_j)^2-gamma*log(sum_predecessors exp(-R/gamma))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Soft-DTW replaces the hard minimum over alignment alternatives with a log-sum-exp soft minimum. Many paths contribute to the result, and the gradient with respect to local costs gives expected cell occupancy. The score is a smooth objective and may be negative or favor a point different from the input itself.

How the calculation works

  1. Form the squared local discrepancy table and choose gamma=0.7.
  2. Use -gamma*log(sum(exp(-previous/gamma))) in each DP update.
  3. Propagate derivatives backward to display expected alignment occupancy across multiple paths.
  4. Read the signed soft objective without choosing a unique path.

Interpretation and limits

  • Gamma controls the spread over alignments; this example uses squared local costs and no path band.
  • Raw soft-DTW can be negative and has an entropic self-bias; do not treat it as a conventional nonnegative distance.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

Soft-DTW 用 log-sum-exp 软最小值替代路径选择中的硬最小值。多个路径共同贡献目标值，对局部代价求导得到每个单元格的期望占据权重。它是平滑目标，可以为负，也可能不在输入自身处取得最小值。

计算步骤

  1. 构造平方局部误差表，设平滑参数 gamma=0.7。
  2. 每步动态规划使用 -gamma*log(sum(exp(-previous/gamma)))。
  3. 反向传播导数，显示多个路径共同形成的期望对齐占据权重。
  4. 读取有符号的软目标值，不选择唯一对齐路径。

解释与使用边界

  • Gamma 控制路径权重的分散程度；本例使用平方局部代价，不加路径窗口。
  • 原始 soft-DTW 可以为负并具有熵正则化自偏差，不能直接作为普通非负距离理解。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "gamma": 0.7,
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "self_x": -4.542304721573488,
  "self_y": -4.93660871304499,
  "cross": -4.4439633238876635
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Cuturi and Blondel (2017): Soft-DTW; Blondel, Mensch and Vert (2021): corrected divergences

Soft-DTW divergence soft_dtw_divergenceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative divergence: 0.29549339 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

D_gamma(x,y)=sDTW_gamma(x,y)-0.5*sDTW_gamma(x,x)-0.5*sDTW_gamma(y,y)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

The corrected soft-DTW divergence subtracts self-alignment terms from the cross-series soft score. This removes its nonzero self-cost by construction. The animation shows both the diffuse alignment occupancy and the three-term correction; theoretical nonnegativity and identification results depend on the ground-cost assumptions.

How the calculation works

  1. Form the squared local discrepancy table and choose gamma=0.7.
  2. Use -gamma*log(sum(exp(-previous/gamma))) in each DP update.
  3. Propagate derivatives backward to display expected alignment occupancy across multiple paths.
  4. Subtract half of softDTW(x,x) and half of softDTW(y,y).

Interpretation and limits

  • Gamma controls the spread over alignments; this example uses squared local costs and no path band.
  • A divergence is not automatically a metric; its properties depend on the ground cost and it need not satisfy the triangle inequality.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

修正 soft-DTW 散度从交叉序列分数中扣除两个自对齐项，因此相同输入的自代价按构造归零。动图展示分散的对齐占据权重和三项修正；非负性与可识别性的理论结论依赖局部代价条件。

计算步骤

  1. 构造平方局部误差表，设平滑参数 gamma=0.7。
  2. 每步动态规划使用 -gamma*log(sum(exp(-previous/gamma)))。
  3. 反向传播导数，显示多个路径共同形成的期望对齐占据权重。
  4. 分别扣除 softDTW(x,x) 和 softDTW(y,y) 的一半。

解释与使用边界

  • Gamma 控制路径权重的分散程度；本例使用平方局部代价，不加路径窗口。
  • 散度并不自动成为度量；其性质依赖局部代价，也不必满足三角不等式。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "gamma": 0.7,
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "self_x": -4.542304721573488,
  "self_y": -4.93660871304499,
  "cross": -4.4439633238876635
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Cuturi and Blondel (2017): Soft-DTW; Blondel, Mensch and Vert (2021): corrected divergences

LCSS matched fraction lcss_similarityChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative similarity: 0.9 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · lcss_similarity

L_ij=L_(i-1,j-1)+1 if |x_i-y_j|<=epsilon, else max(L_(i-1,j),L_(i,j-1)); s=L_nm/min(n,m)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

LCSS counts an order-preserving set of sufficiently close sample pairs. Unmatched samples may be skipped, so the result describes matched coverage rather than the magnitude of every residual. The animation uses a tolerance of 0.35 and divides the match count by the shorter sequence length.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Count tolerance matches and carry forward the larger match count when skipping.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • Tolerance changes which pairs count as matches. Missing subsequences may be ignored by the shorter-length normalization.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

LCSS 寻找保持先后顺序且足够接近的观测配对。未匹配点可以跳过，因此结果反映匹配覆盖率，不累加全部数值误差。本例容差为 0.35，并以较短序列长度归一化匹配数量。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 累计容差内匹配，跳过观测时保留较大的匹配计数。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 容差改变哪些配对算匹配；以较短长度归一化可能忽略较长序列中未匹配的部分。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "match count / min(n,m)",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "epsilon": 0.35
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: Longest Common Subsequence; EchoTime low-level similarity implementations

One minus LCSS similarity lcss_distanceChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.1 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · lcss_distance

d_LCSS=1-LCSS_epsilon(x,y)/min(n,m)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

This distance-like transform is one minus the normalized LCSS match count. It retains the order-preserving, tolerance-based matching rule. The final conversion reverses the direction of the score without restoring information discarded by skipped points or thresholding; different sequences can therefore receive zero distance.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Count tolerance matches and carry forward the larger match count when skipping.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Convert the count to one minus its shorter-length-normalized value.

Interpretation and limits

  • One minus a similarity need not satisfy metric axioms. Zero here means full tolerance-based coverage of the shorter sequence.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

该距离形式等于一减归一化 LCSS 匹配数量，仍采用保持顺序的容差匹配。最后的变换只反转分数方向，无法恢复跳过观测或阈值判断丢失的信息，因此不同序列也可能得到零距离。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 累计容差内匹配，跳过观测时保留较大的匹配计数。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 将匹配数量除以较短长度，再用一减去该比例。

解释与使用边界

  • 一减相似度不保证满足度量公理；这里的零值表示较短序列在容差内被完全覆盖。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "match count / min(n,m)",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "epsilon": 0.35
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: Longest Common Subsequence; EchoTime low-level similarity implementations

Edit distance on real sequences edrChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.2 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · edr_distance

D_ij=min(D_(i-1,j)+1,D_(i,j-1)+1,D_(i-1,j-1)+1[|x_i-y_j|>epsilon]); d=D_nm/max(n,m)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

EDR replaces the size of a sample discrepancy with a thresholded substitution cost. A nearby pair costs zero, a mismatching substitution costs one, and insertions or deletions cost one. Dynamic programming finds the least expensive edit sequence; this example reports EchoTime's length-normalized default.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Choose the cheapest substitution, insertion or deletion for each prefix.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • The amplitude of a mismatch beyond epsilon is discarded. Normalization and epsilon must be specified when comparing implementations.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

EDR 把观测差异转成阈值替换代价：相近配对为零，不匹配替换为一，插入和删除也为一。动态规划寻找费用最低的编辑序列；本例采用 EchoTime 默认的长度归一化输出。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 逐个前缀选择费用最低的替换、插入或删除。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 超过 epsilon 后的误差幅度被丢弃；比较不同实现时须明确容差与归一化。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "edit count / max(n,m)",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "epsilon": 0.35
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level similarity implementations; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

SWALE rewards and gap costs swale_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative alignment score: 17 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

S_ij=2+S_(i-1,j-1) for a tolerance match; otherwise max(S_(i-1,j)-0.5,S_(i,j-1)-0.5)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

SWALE scores tolerance-based matches with a reward and unmatched observations with a gap penalty. Its recurrence combines the number of accepted matches with the price of omitted observations. This example uses reward 2, gap score -0.5 and epsilon 0.35; the raw score is not normalized to a probability.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Reward a tolerance match; otherwise choose the less costly gap transition.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • Rewards, gap penalties and sequence length influence the score scale. This implements the paper's scoring recurrence, not its FTSE acceleration.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

SWALE 对容差内的匹配给奖励，对未匹配的观测施加间隙惩罚。递推同时考虑接受的匹配数量与省略观测的费用。本例奖励为 2、间隙分数为 -0.5、容差为 0.35；原始分数没有归一化为概率。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 对容差匹配给予奖励，否则选择费用较低的间隙转移。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 奖励、间隙惩罚和序列长度都会影响分数量级；本例演示论文的计分递推，没有实现 FTSE 加速。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "none",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "epsilon": 0.35,
  "match_reward": 2.0,
  "gap_score": -0.5
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Morse and Patel (2007), Definition 5.1

Edit distance with a real gap erpChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 1.7 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · erp_distance

D_ij=min(D_(i-1,j-1)+|x_i-y_j|,D_(i-1,j)+|x_i-g|,D_(i,j-1)+|y_j-g|)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

ERP charges the numerical cost of matching observations or matching one observation to a fixed gap value. The animation compares substitution with deletion and insertion at each prefix. Here the gap is 0.5 and the univariate local norm is absolute difference, matching EchoTime's explicit low-level calculation.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Compare matching x to y with matching either observation to gap g=0.5.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • Gap choice changes the cost. If the gap value is also a valid observation, distinct raw lists can have zero ERP distance.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

ERP 对观测之间的替换以及观测与固定间隙值之间的匹配收取数值代价。动图逐个前缀比较替换、删除与插入。本例间隙值为 0.5，单变量局部范数为绝对差，与 EchoTime 底层计算一致。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 比较 x 与 y 的匹配，以及任一观测与间隙 g=0.5 的匹配。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 间隙值选择会改变代价；若间隙值也属于合法观测，不同原始列表可以得到零 ERP 距离。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "none",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "gap_value": 0.5
}

Move, split and merge msmReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 2.6 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

C(v,a,b)=c if v lies between a,b; else c+min(|v-a|,|v-b|); D=min(move,split,merge)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

MSM transforms one ordered sequence through move, split and merge operations. Moving costs the absolute change in value. Split/merge costs depend on a base cost and neighboring values, so the recurrence uses local context instead of a fixed gap symbol. The animation displays its cumulative operation costs.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Compare a move with context-dependent split and merge costs.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • This is a univariate definition with c=0.6. Multivariate dependent and independent generalizations require separate conventions.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

MSM 通过移动、分裂和合并操作变换有序序列。移动费用是数值的绝对变化；分裂与合并费用取决于基础费用和邻近数值，因此递推使用局部上下文而非固定间隙符号。动图展示这些操作的累计费用。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 比较移动费用与依赖上下文的分裂、合并费用。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 本例是 c=0.6 的单变量定义；多变量的联合式与独立式推广须另行指定。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "none",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "c": 0.6
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: aeon: Move-Split-Merge recurrence

Time warp edit distance twedChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 6.7 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · twed_distance

D=min(Dprev+|x_i-y_j|+|x_(i-1)-y_(j-1)|+nu*(|t_i-s_j|+|t_(i-1)-s_(j-1)|),delete_x,delete_y)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

TWED combines edit penalties, consecutive-value changes and timestamp discrepancies. A match compares both the current pair and the preceding pair; deletions also include elapsed-time stiffness. The animation uses the zero-value, zero-time sentinel and infinite leading boundaries from EchoTime's corrected recurrence.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Combine value changes, time stiffness and deletion penalties in the minimum-cost recurrence.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • Positive stiffness, timestamps, the sentinel and boundary conventions matter to metric claims. A constrained or resampled approximation does not automatically inherit them.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

TWED 联合考虑编辑惩罚、相邻数值变化和时间戳差异。匹配同时比较当前配对与前一个配对；删除还包括经过时间的刚度代价。动图使用 EchoTime 修正递推中的零值零时刻哨兵及无穷边界。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 在最小代价递推中结合数值变化、时间刚度和删除惩罚。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 正刚度、时间戳、哨兵和边界约定都是度量结论的条件；加约束或重采样近似不自动继承这些性质。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "none",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "lambda_": 0.4,
  "nu": 0.2,
  "t_x": [
    1.0,
    1.8,
    2.9000000000000004,
    3.8000000000000003,

Definition sources / 定义来源: Marteau: TWED recurrence and stiffness; EchoTime low-level similarity implementations

Discrete Frechet bottleneck discrete_frechetReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.2 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

F_ij=max(|x_i-y_j|,min(F_(i-1,j),F_(i,j-1),F_(i-1,j-1)))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Discrete Frechet comparison minimizes the largest local separation along a monotone traversal. This differs from objectives that add every local mismatch: one difficult pair can determine the bottleneck. Here observations are points on the value axis, with order preserved and no extra time coordinate in the local norm.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Keep the larger of the present separation and the smallest predecessor bottleneck.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • Repeated vertices can make distinct raw vertex lists indistinguishable. Adding time as a coordinate changes the geometry and its scale.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

离散 Fréchet 比较最小化单调遍历过程中最大的局部距离。单个困难配对可能决定最终瓶颈。本例将观测视为数值轴上的点，保留其顺序，但局部范数不额外包含时间坐标。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 在当前距离和最小前驱瓶颈之间取较大者。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 重复顶点可能使不同原始顶点列表无法区分；加入时间坐标会改变几何与尺度。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "none",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Eiter and Mannila (1994): discrete Frechet distance

Global sequence alignment needleman_wunsch_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative alignment score: 17 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

S_ij=max(S_(i-1,j-1)+s_ij,S_(i-1,j)-0.5,S_(i,j-1)-0.5); s_ij=2 if close else -1

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

This numerical Needleman-Wunsch illustration aligns both full sequences using an explicit match/mismatch rule and linear gap costs. The entire prefix table contributes to a global solution ending at its bottom-right cell. The time-series scoring rule is specified here, rather than implied by the algorithm name.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Maximize the global score with a match/mismatch or a linear gap transition.
  3. Trace the selected predecessor operations from the complete-prefix state.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • The biological algorithm does not fix a numerical time-series substitution score. This educational adaptation uses epsilon=0.35 and linear, not affine, gaps.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

该数值 Needleman-Wunsch 示例使用明确的匹配／不匹配规则与线性间隙费用，对齐两条完整序列。全局解终止于前缀表右下角。本卡明确指定时间序列的计分规则，不将其视为算法名称隐含的唯一选择。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 使用匹配／不匹配或线性间隙转移，最大化全局分数。
  3. 从完整前缀状态沿选定的前驱操作回溯。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 原始序列算法不规定数值时间序列的替换分数；本教学改写使用 epsilon=0.35 和线性间隙，不使用仿射间隙。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "none",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "epsilon": 0.35,
  "match_reward": 2.0,
  "gap_score": -0.5,
  "mismatch_score": -1.0
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Biopython: global and local sequence alignment; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Local sequence alignment smith_waterman_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative alignment score: 18 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

S_ij=max(0,S_(i-1,j-1)+s_ij,S_(i-1,j)-0.5,S_(i,j-1)-0.5); result=max_ij S_ij

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Smith-Waterman allows an alignment to restart at zero, so it can select matching internal subsequences while leaving unrelated ends unused. This explicit numerical example uses the same threshold substitution scores as the global card. The best cell may occur anywhere, and traceback stops when the score returns to zero.

How the calculation works

  1. Set the stated local scoring rule, tolerance and boundary conditions.
  2. Maximize the score with an additional zero restart option at every cell.
  3. Start at the maximum score and stop traceback at zero.
  4. Read the final score with the normalization explicitly stated below.

Interpretation and limits

  • A high local alignment score does not establish whole-series similarity. Substitution and gap choices determine which segment wins.
  • The small arrays are an algorithm illustration, not evidence of retrieval or classification quality.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

Smith-Waterman 允许分数从零重新开始，因此能选择内部匹配子序列，并不使用无关的两端。本数值示例采用与全局卡片相同的阈值替换计分。最优单元格可以位于任意位置，回溯在零分处停止。

计算步骤

  1. 设置明确的局部计分规则、容差与边界条件。
  2. 在每个单元格增加从零开始选项，并最大化分数。
  3. 从最大分数处开始回溯，在零分处停止。
  4. 按下方明确的归一化约定读取最终分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 较高的局部对齐分数不能证明整段序列相似；替换与间隙设定决定获选片段。
  • 这些小数组用于解释计算过程，不构成检索或分类性能证据。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "normalization": "none",
  "local_norm": "absolute difference",
  "epsilon": 0.35,
  "match_reward": 2.0,
  "gap_score": -0.5,
  "mismatch_score": -1.0
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Biopython: global and local sequence alignment; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

DTW on local shape descriptors shape_dtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.97467943 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

phi(x_i)=[x_(i-1),x_i,x_(i+1)] with edge repetition; d=DTW(phi(x),phi(y))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

shapeDTW compares short neighborhoods around samples so that a match reflects local context. Here each descriptor contains three raw values with edge repetition, and DTW minimizes squared descriptor differences. The animation reports the descriptor-space objective, an explicit variant rather than a raw-signal score recomputed along its path.

How the calculation works

  1. Construct a three-sample raw neighborhood descriptor at each index, repeating edge values.
  2. Compute local squared discrepancies; apply the indicated weights or admissibility mask.
  3. At each admissible cell, add its local cost to the smallest predecessor cost.
  4. Trace one minimizing path and take the square root of its accumulated objective.

Interpretation and limits

  • Descriptor radius, scaling and the choice to rescore the final path in raw space all change the method. No trained descriptor is used here.
  • Signal scale, path constraints and final normalization must match before distances from different tools are compared.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

shapeDTW 用观测周围的短邻域描述局部形状。本例将三个原始数值组成描述符，边界使用重复填充，然后最小化描述符之间的平方差。动图报告描述符空间的目标值，不是沿该路径重新计算的原始信号距离。

计算步骤

  1. 逐点构造三个采样值的原始邻域描述符，在边界重复端点数值。
  2. 计算局部平方误差，并施加指定权重或允许匹配的掩码。
  3. 在每个允许单元格，把局部代价加到三个前驱的最小累计代价上。
  4. 回溯一条最小化目标的路径，并对其累计目标值取平方根。

解释与使用边界

  • 邻域半径、缩放以及是否在原空间重新计分都会改变结果；本例没有训练描述符。
  • 比较不同工具的数值之前，须统一信号尺度、路径约束和最终归一化。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "local_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "final_square_root": true,
  "normalization": "none",
  "descriptor": true,
  "descriptor_radius": 1,
  "padding": "edge repetition",
  "reported_cost": "descriptor-space"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Zhao and Itti: shapeDTW; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Independent-channel DTW independent_dtwReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.41068967 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d_independent(X,Y) = (d_base(X_channel1,Y_channel1)+d_base(X_channel2,Y_channel2))/2

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Independent DTW computes a separate alignment problem for every channel, then averages the resulting distances. The two channels in this example prefer different correspondences. Their paths are shown separately, making explicit that averaging final scores does not impose a shared multivariate warping path.

How the calculation works

  1. Keep matching channel identities and define channelwise preprocessing.
  2. Solve the stated DTW problem independently for channel one and channel two.
  3. Retain each channel's own alignment; different channels may select different time matches.
  4. Take the arithmetic mean of channel distances, with equal weights in this example.

Interpretation and limits

  • Independent warping can break simultaneity across channels; use a shared-path definition when simultaneous events must remain coupled.
  • Channel scale and aggregation convention matter. This atlas uses a mean; some libraries use a sum.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

独立通道 DTW 为每个通道分别求解对齐问题，再平均最终距离。本例两个通道偏好的配对不同，动图分别显示各自路径，因此可直接看到平均分数不等于施加共享的多变量扭曲路径。

计算步骤

  1. 保持通道对应关系，并明确各通道预处理。
  2. 分别求解通道一和通道二的 DTW 问题。
  3. 保留各通道自身的对齐；不同通道可以选择不同时间匹配。
  4. 对通道距离取算术平均；本例使用相等权重。

解释与使用边界

  • 各通道独立扭曲可能破坏通道间同时性；要求同步事件保持耦合时，应选择共享路径定义。
  • 通道尺度和聚合约定会影响结果；本图谱使用平均值，有些库使用求和。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "channels_x": [
    [
      0.2,
      0.4,
      1.1,
      2.4,
      3.2,
      2.5,
      1.2,
      0.3,
      -0.4,
      -0.1
    ],
    [
      0.2,
      0.5,
      1.2,
      2.2,
      2.9,
      2.1,
      1.1,
      0.1,
      -0.2,
      0.1
    ]
  ],
  "channels_y": [
    [
      0.1,
      0.3,
      0.5,
      1.2,
      2.6,
      3.1,
      2.3,
      1.1,
      0.2,
      -0.3
    ],
    [
      0.4,
      1.1,
      2.3,
      3.0,
      2.0,
      1.0,
      0.2,
      -0.1,
      0.0,
      0.2
    ]
  ],
  "base": "dtw",
  "aggregation": "arithmetic mean",
  "channel_values": [
    0.4472135954999581,
    0.37416573867739417
  ]
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: DTW definitions and path constraints; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Independent-channel ERP independent_erpReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 1.5 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d_independent(X,Y) = (d_base(X_channel1,Y_channel1)+d_base(X_channel2,Y_channel2))/2

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Independent ERP computes a separate alignment problem for every channel, then averages the resulting distances. The two channels in this example prefer different correspondences. Their paths are shown separately, making explicit that averaging final scores does not impose a shared multivariate warping path.

How the calculation works

  1. Keep matching channel identities and define channelwise preprocessing.
  2. Solve the stated ERP problem independently for channel one and channel two.
  3. Retain each channel's own alignment; different channels may select different time matches.
  4. Take the arithmetic mean of channel distances, with equal weights in this example.

Interpretation and limits

  • Independent warping can break simultaneity across channels; use a shared-path definition when simultaneous events must remain coupled.
  • Channel scale and aggregation convention matter. This atlas uses a mean; some libraries use a sum.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

独立通道 ERP 为每个通道分别求解对齐问题，再平均最终距离。本例两个通道偏好的配对不同，动图分别显示各自路径，因此可直接看到平均分数不等于施加共享的多变量扭曲路径。

计算步骤

  1. 保持通道对应关系，并明确各通道预处理。
  2. 分别求解通道一和通道二的 ERP 问题。
  3. 保留各通道自身的对齐；不同通道可以选择不同时间匹配。
  4. 对通道距离取算术平均；本例使用相等权重。

解释与使用边界

  • 各通道独立扭曲可能破坏通道间同时性；要求同步事件保持耦合时，应选择共享路径定义。
  • 通道尺度和聚合约定会影响结果；本图谱使用平均值，有些库使用求和。

Kernel constructions / 核函数构造 10
Linear inner-product kernel linear_kernelReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative kernel score: 22.68 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

K(x,y)=sum_i x_i*y_i

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

The linear kernel adds products of corresponding coordinates. Its animation shows which indices contribute positively or negatively before adding them into the inner product. It preserves amplitude and offset effects unless preprocessing removes them, and its scale grows with vector energy.

How the calculation works

  1. Use the same aligned coordinate system for both vectors.
  2. Compute and sum the coordinatewise contributions shown in the plot.
  3. Apply the specified scalar kernel transformation to that complete sum.
  4. Read the pair score; kernel magnitude is not a calibrated similarity probability.

Interpretation and limits

  • An inner product is not bounded by one and can be negative; normalization would produce a different kernel or cosine score.
  • No local time warping is performed by this aligned-vector construction.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

线性核将对应坐标的乘积相加。动图先展示各位置的正负贡献，再累加成内积。若预处理没有消除幅度与偏移，它们会保留在结果中；分数量级随向量能量变化。

计算步骤

  1. 对两个向量使用同一个已对齐坐标系。
  2. 计算图示逐坐标贡献并求和。
  3. 对完整求和结果施加指定的标量核变换。
  4. 读取成对分数；核函数量级不是校准后的相似概率。

解释与使用边界

  • 内积没有一的上界，也可以为负；归一化会产生不同的核或余弦分数。
  • 此已对齐向量构造不执行局部时间扭曲。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "preprocessing": "none"
}

K(x,y)=(0.1*dot(x,y)+1)^3

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

The polynomial kernel first computes an inner product, then applies a shifted integer power. Here degree three creates nonlinear interactions implicitly without constructing every product feature. The animation separates the coordinate sum from its nonlinear expansion, so the large final magnitude is not mistaken for a probability.

How the calculation works

  1. Use the same aligned coordinate system for both vectors.
  2. Compute and sum the coordinatewise contributions shown in the plot.
  3. Apply the specified scalar kernel transformation to that complete sum.
  4. Read the pair score; kernel magnitude is not a calibrated similarity probability.

Interpretation and limits

  • The usual positive-semidefinite guarantee uses a nonnegative scale/offset and integer degree. Large norms or degrees can dominate numerically.
  • No local time warping is performed by this aligned-vector construction.

计算步骤

  2. 计算图示逐坐标贡献并求和。
  3. 对完整求和结果施加指定的标量核变换。
  4. 读取成对分数；核函数量级不是校准后的相似概率。

The formula is commonly called a sigmoid kernel, but arbitrary scale and offset choices do not guarantee a positive-semidefinite Gram matrix.

How the calculation works

  2. Compute and sum the coordinatewise contributions shown in the plot.
  3. Apply the specified scalar kernel transformation to that complete sum.

Interpretation and limits

  • No local time warping is performed by this aligned-vector construction.

  • No local time warping is performed by this aligned-vector construction.

Exponential transform of DTW dtw_rbf_kernelReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative kernel score: 0.86935824 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

K=exp(-0.7*DTW(x,y)^2), DTW uses squared costs then square root

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

This construction first selects the minimum-cost DTW path and then exponentially transforms its squared distance. The animation shows the path calculation before the score conversion. Although the formula resembles a Gaussian kernel, replacing Euclidean distance by DTW does not automatically preserve positive semidefiniteness.

How the calculation works

  1. Build squared local costs and a monotone DTW recurrence.
  2. Select the path with the least accumulated discrepancy.
  3. Use that accumulated cost as DTW squared in exp(-gamma*cost).
  4. Interpret the decreasing score with gamma=0.7 and the stated local norm.

Interpretation and limits

  • A DTW-based exponential may produce an indefinite Gram matrix. Do not infer kernel-method guarantees from its name.
  • The value changes with the path band, local cost and distance normalization.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

该构造先选择最低代价 DTW 路径，再对距离平方做指数变换。动图先展示路径计算，再展示分数转换。将欧氏距离替换成 DTW 不自动保留高斯核的半正定性。

计算步骤

  1. 构造平方局部代价与单调 DTW 递推。
  2. 选择累计差异最小的路径。
  3. 把该累计代价作为 DTW 平方代入 exp(-gamma*cost)。
  4. 结合 gamma=0.7 和明确的局部范数理解该递减分数。

解释与使用边界

  • DTW 指数形式可能得到不定 Gram 矩阵；不能从名称推断核方法保证。
  • 路径窗口、局部代价和距离归一化都会改变结果。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "gamma": 0.7,
  "base_distance": 0.4472135954999581,
  "squared_distance": 0.20000000000000015
}

Exponential transform of SBD sbd_kernelReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative kernel score: 0.82362 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

K=exp(-2*(1-max_lag NCC_lag(z(x),z(y))))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

SBD selects the largest normalized cross-correlation over global shifts, then subtracts it from one. This atlas construction maps that SBD value through exp(-2*SBD). The lag sweep makes its shift tolerance visible, while its full-series normalization distinguishes it from overlap-normalized Pearson correlation.

How the calculation works

  1. Z-normalize both series using their own full-series mean and standard deviation.
  2. Evaluate zero-padded cross-correlation at every global lag.
  3. Set SBD to one minus the largest full-energy-normalized correlation.
  4. Apply exp(-2*SBD) to obtain the stated kernel-like score.

Interpretation and limits

  • Maximizing over shifts and exponentiating a distance-like score do not establish positive semidefiniteness.
  • Only one global lag is chosen. Constant inputs have undefined statistical normalization and need an explicit software convention.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

SBD 在全局平移中选择最大的归一化互相关，再用一减去该值。本图谱构造进一步计算 exp(-2*SBD)。动图逐个扫描滞后，展示平移容忍性；采用完整序列能量归一化，与重叠区域 Pearson 相关不同。

计算步骤

  1. 用各自完整序列的均值和标准差进行 z 标准化。
  2. 在每个全局滞后处计算零填充互相关。
  3. 令 SBD 等于一减完整能量归一化相关的最大值。
  4. 应用 exp(-2*SBD)，得到明确约定的核形式分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 对平移取最大值再指数化距离形式分数，不能证明半正定性。
  • 只选择一个全局滞后；常数输入的统计归一化未定义，需要明确的软件约定。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "gamma": 2.0,
  "normalize": true,
  "NCC_normalization": "full-series L2 energies",
  "best_lag": -1,
  "sbd": 0.09702301325884444
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level similarity implementations; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Exponential soft-DTW divergence soft_dtw_kernelReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative kernel score: 0.65564656 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

K=exp(-D_gamma/gamma); D_gamma=sDTW(x,y)-0.5*sDTW(x,x)-0.5*sDTW(y,y)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

This imported atlas name denotes an exponential of the self-corrected soft-DTW divergence. It first includes contributions from multiple alignments, subtracts both self terms, then applies exp(-D/gamma). This explicit construction is separate from directly exponentiating raw soft-DTW or choosing a modified local kernel for GAK.

How the calculation works

  1. Compute cross and self soft-DTW objectives with the same gamma and local cost.
  2. Inspect the diffuse occupancy of alternative alignments.
  3. Subtract the self terms to obtain the corrected divergence D.
  4. Evaluate exp(-D/gamma) using gamma=0.7.

Interpretation and limits

  • A corrected divergence does not by itself prove that its exponential is a positive-semidefinite kernel for an arbitrary ground cost.
  • This gamma is used for both soft alignment and the final exponential; a separate outer bandwidth would define another variant.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

本图谱名称对应自修正 soft-DTW 散度的指数形式。先综合多个对齐路径，减去两个自对齐项，再计算 exp(-D/gamma)。该明确构造区别于直接指数化原始 soft-DTW，也区别于为 GAK 选择修正局部核。

计算步骤

  1. 使用相同 gamma 和局部代价计算交叉及自对齐 soft-DTW。
  2. 检查备选对齐的分散占据权重。
  3. 扣除自对齐项得到修正散度 D。
  4. 使用 gamma=0.7 计算 exp(-D/gamma)。

解释与使用边界

  • 修正散度本身不能证明对任意局部代价，其指数形式都是半正定核。
  • 这里同一个 gamma 同时控制软对齐与最终指数变换；若另设外层带宽，则得到另一变体。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "gamma": 0.7,
  "ground_cost": "squared Euclidean",
  "divergence": 0.2954933934215753
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Blondel, Mensch and Vert (2021): corrected divergences; Cuturi and Blondel (2017): Soft-DTW; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Global alignment kernel gakReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative kernel score: 3739.5094 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

K_ij=kappa_ij*(K_(i-1,j)+K_(i,j-1)+K_(i-1,j-1)); kappa=phi/(2-phi), K_00=1

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

The Global Alignment Kernel adds products of local similarities over all monotone alignment paths. The dynamic program uses a sum rather than choosing a minimum-cost predecessor. Here the local kernel is phi/(2-phi), with phi a Gaussian, following a sufficient positive-definiteness construction; the displayed result is unnormalized.

How the calculation works

  1. Build phi=exp(-squared_cost/(2*sigma^2)) with sigma=1, then kappa=phi/(2-phi).
  2. Multiply each local kappa by the sum of its three predecessor path weights.
  3. Accumulate every monotone alignment; do not replace the sum with a maximum or minimum.
  4. Read the unnormalized path sum; optional self-normalization is a separate step.

Interpretation and limits

  • An arbitrary local similarity or hard path restriction does not inherit the stated positive-definiteness construction.
  • Raw path sums depend strongly on sequence length and can overflow for long inputs; log-domain computation and self-normalization are distinct remedies.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

全局对齐核将所有单调对齐路径上的局部相似度乘积相加。动态规划对前驱求和，不选择最低代价前驱。本例局部核为 phi/(2-phi)，其中 phi 是高斯核，对应一个充分的正定性构造；输出未做自相似归一化。

计算步骤

  1. 用 sigma=1 构造 phi=exp(-平方代价/(2*sigma^2))，再令 kappa=phi/(2-phi)。
  2. 每个局部 kappa 乘以三个前驱路径权重之和。
  3. 累加全部单调对齐，不能将求和换成最大或最小值。
  4. 读取未归一化路径和；可选的自相似归一化是另一个步骤。

解释与使用边界

  • 任意局部相似度或硬路径限制都不自动继承上述正定性构造。
  • 原始路径和强烈依赖长度，长输入可能溢出；对数域计算和自相似归一化是不同处理方式。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "sigma": 1.0,
  "local_kernel": "phi/(2-phi)",
  "normalization": "none",
  "hard_path_band": null,
  "epsilon": 0.0,
  "raw_gak": 3739.5094447576153
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Cuturi (2011): local-kernel conditions for global alignment; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Log global alignment score lgakReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative kernel score: 8.2267097 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

log(K_GA+1e-12), K_GA=sum_paths product(phi/(2-phi)), phi=exp(-(x_i-y_j)^2/2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Log GAK applies a logarithm to the positive sum of alignment weights. The underlying computation still uses sum-product dynamic programming, so all admissible paths contribute. This card uses Cuturi's modified Gaussian local kernel and reports log of the unnormalized path sum, with a stated tiny epsilon for numerical definition.

How the calculation works

  1. Build phi=exp(-squared_cost/(2*sigma^2)) with sigma=1, then kappa=phi/(2-phi).
  2. Multiply each local kappa by the sum of its three predecessor path weights.
  3. Accumulate every monotone alignment; do not replace the sum with a maximum or minimum.
  4. Apply log(K+epsilon) to compress the positive output scale.

Interpretation and limits

  • The logarithm does not generally preserve positive semidefiniteness; log GAK is a score transformation, not an automatic Mercer kernel.
  • Raw path sums depend strongly on sequence length and can overflow for long inputs; log-domain computation and self-normalization are distinct remedies.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

Log GAK 对正的对齐权重总和取对数。底层仍使用求和—乘积动态规划，因此全部允许路径共同贡献。本卡采用 Cuturi 的修正高斯局部核，报告未归一化路径和的对数，并明确微小 epsilon 的数值约定。

计算步骤

  1. 用 sigma=1 构造 phi=exp(-平方代价/(2*sigma^2))，再令 kappa=phi/(2-phi)。
  2. 每个局部 kappa 乘以三个前驱路径权重之和。
  3. 累加全部单调对齐，不能将求和换成最大或最小值。
  4. 应用 log(K+epsilon) 压缩正输出的量级。

解释与使用边界

  • 取对数一般不保留半正定性；log GAK 是分数变换，不自动成为 Mercer 核。
  • 原始路径和强烈依赖长度，长输入可能溢出；对数域计算和自相似归一化是不同处理方式。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "sigma": 1.0,
  "local_kernel": "phi/(2-phi)",
  "normalization": "none",
  "hard_path_band": null,
  "epsilon": 1e-12,
  "raw_gak": 3739.5094447576153
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Cuturi (2011): local-kernel conditions for global alignment; EchoTime imported atlas: named formula conventions

Weighted lag correlation score weighted_ncc_kernelReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative kernel score: 0.28020318 — The explicit definition and parameters shown on this card.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

K=sum_l w_l*NCC_l(z(x),z(y)); w_l proportional to exp(-l^2/(2*2^2))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

This explicitly specified score averages normalized cross-correlations across lags using fixed symmetric weights. Nearby shifts receive more weight than large shifts; no winning lag is selected. The animation separates correlation, lag preference, multiplication and accumulation, making clear how the score differs from maximum NCC.

How the calculation works

  1. Z-normalize the input and compute full-energy NCC for all zero-padded shifts.
  2. Assign Gaussian lag weights with sigma=2 and normalize their sum to one.
  3. Multiply every lag correlation by its corresponding weight.
  4. Sum the contributions; this score averages lag evidence rather than selecting one lag.

Interpretation and limits

  • Weighting rules are part of the definition. This generic atlas formula does not supply a positive-semidefinite guarantee for arbitrary lag weights.
  • Full-series normalization, zero padding and centering differ from overlap-specific correlations. Constant inputs need a separate convention.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

该明确指定的分数使用固定对称权重对各滞后的归一化互相关求加权平均。较小位移得到较大权重，不选择获胜滞后。动图分别展示相关、滞后偏好、乘法和累计过程，说明其与最大 NCC 的计算区别。

计算步骤

  1. 对输入 z 标准化，并计算所有零填充位移的完整能量 NCC。
  2. 设置 sigma=2 的高斯滞后权重，并使权重总和为一。
  3. 将每个滞后相关乘以对应权重。
  4. 累计贡献；该分数平均滞后证据，不选取单个滞后。

解释与使用边界

  • 权重规则是定义的一部分；通用图谱公式不为任意滞后权重提供半正定保证。
  • 完整序列归一化、零填充和中心化与重叠区域相关不同；常数输入需要另行约定。

Explicit features / 显式特征 18
Basic statistics distance stats_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.3997628 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Represent each series by its mean, population standard deviation, minimum and maximum. These four coordinates summarize level, spread and range; their order in the vector is fixed, while the original temporal order is discarded. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Compute mean, population SD, minimum and maximum in that order.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Permuting observations leaves this vector unchanged, even when temporal behavior changes completely.
  • Features have different scales; this example uses no coordinate weighting. A production scaler must be fitted without test-data leakage.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把每条序列表示为均值、总体标准差、最小值和最大值四个坐标。它们概括水平、波动和范围，坐标顺序固定，但原始时间顺序被舍弃。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 按均值、总体标准差、最小值、最大值的顺序提取特征。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 任意重排序列不会改变该向量，即使时间行为发生显著变化。
  • 各坐标尺度不同；本例不加权。实际使用的缩放器应避免测试数据泄漏。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "features": [
    "mean",
    "std_ddof0",
    "min",
    "max"
  ],
  "coordinate_scaling": "none",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Robust statistics distance robust_stats_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.12641944 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Replace extrema and moments with the lower quartile, median, upper quartile and median absolute deviation. This explicit descriptor emphasizes the central distribution and reduces the influence of isolated extreme values. It does not preserve the timing of those values. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Calculate Q25, median, Q75 and unscaled median absolute deviation.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Robustness concerns these particular statistics, not immunity to arbitrary contamination or distribution shift.
  • Quantiles use linear interpolation and MAD is unscaled; alternative conventions produce different vectors.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

使用下四分位数、中位数、上四分位数和中位绝对偏差构成明确的描述向量。它强调分布的中心部分，降低孤立极端值的影响，同时舍弃这些数值出现的时刻。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 计算 Q25、中位数、Q75 和未经常数缩放的中位绝对偏差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 稳健性针对所选统计量，并不表示可抵抗任意污染或分布变化。
  • 本例分位数采用线性插值，MAD 不乘缩放常数；其他约定会产生不同向量。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "features": [
    "q25",
    "median",
    "q75",
    "unscaled_MAD"
  ],
  "quantile_method": "linear",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}
