Autocorrelation features distance acf_distanceChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.36880413 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · acf_distance

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

Center each series and compare it with delayed versions of itself. Six lag coordinates summarize repeated linear dependence. This example follows EchoTime's adjusted denominator, (n-lag) times the population variance, and omits the always-one lag-zero coordinate. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. At each lag, sum centered lagged products and divide by (n-lag) times variance.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Adjusted high-lag sample estimates need not remain inside [-1,1]; estimates are less reliable with fewer overlapping pairs.
  • ACF drops the mean and amplitude scale and cannot fully identify nonlinear dependence or the original waveform.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

先对序列去均值，再与自身的延迟版本比较。六个滞后坐标描述重复出现的线性依赖。本例采用 EchoTime 的调整分母，即 (n-lag) 乘总体方差，并省略恒为一的零滞后坐标。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 逐个滞后累加去均值后的乘积，除以 (n-lag) 乘方差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 经过样本数调整的高滞后估计不一定落在 [-1,1]，重叠点越少估计越不稳定。
  • ACF 舍弃均值和振幅尺度，无法完整识别非线性依赖或恢复原波形。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "max_lag": 6,
  "denominator": "(n-lag)*population_variance",
  "lag_zero": "omitted",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; statsmodels: ACF denominator and adjusted convention; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Fourier-magnitude cosine similarity spectral_cosine_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative similarity: 0.97817876 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Conceptually covered · spectral_similarity

s(x,y) = dot(phi(x),phi(y)) / (norm(phi(x))*norm(phi(y)))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Project the series onto sine and cosine bases, combine the real and imaginary parts into magnitudes, and compare the resulting frequency bins. The example retains all nine nonnegative-frequency bins of a length-16 real transform, including the DC bin. Cosine compares vector direction and is defined here for nonzero feature vectors.

How the calculation works

  1. Compute the unnormalized real FFT and retain the magnitude of each bin.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Normalize by both vector lengths and sum coordinate products to obtain cosine similarity.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Magnitude discards phase; a circular shift changes Fourier phase while preserving these magnitudes.
  • The DC coordinate reflects the mean, and raw magnitudes depend on record length and amplitude. Matching sampling rates and frequency grids is required.
  • This example compares unweighted nonnegative-frequency rFFT magnitudes. Comparing a full FFT, or weighting interior rFFT magnitudes by sqrt(2), gives a different distance/cosine convention.
  • Cosine ignores positive rescaling of the entire feature vector; 1-cosine is generally not a metric, and zero vectors require an explicit policy.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把序列投影到正弦、余弦基上，将实部和虚部合成为幅值，再比较频率坐标。本例保留长度 16 实数变换的全部九个非负频率坐标，包括直流分量。 余弦比较向量方向，本例特征向量均非零。

计算步骤

  1. 计算未归一化的实数 FFT，保留各频率坐标的模。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 用两个向量的长度归一化坐标乘积之和，得到余弦相似度。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 幅值表示舍弃相位；循环平移改变傅里叶相位，却保留这些幅值。
  • 直流坐标反映均值，原始幅值依赖记录长度与振幅；比较时需要一致的采样率和频率网格。
  • 本例比较未加权的非负频率 rFFT 幅值。比较完整 FFT 或将内部 rFFT 幅值乘 sqrt(2)，对应不同的距离与余弦约定。
  • 余弦忽略整个特征向量的正比例缩放；1-cosine 通常不满足度量公理，零向量还需明确处理规则。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "fft": "rfft",
  "normalization": "backward",
  "n_bins": 9,
  "include_DC": true,
  "comparison": "cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: real-input discrete Fourier transform; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Fourier-magnitude cosine distance spectral_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.021821236 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d(x,y) = 1 - dot(phi(x),phi(y)) / (norm(phi(x))*norm(phi(y)))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Project the series onto sine and cosine bases, combine the real and imaginary parts into magnitudes, and compare the resulting frequency bins. The example retains all nine nonnegative-frequency bins of a length-16 real transform, including the DC bin. Cosine compares vector direction and is defined here for nonzero feature vectors.

How the calculation works

  1. Compute the unnormalized real FFT and retain the magnitude of each bin.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Compute cosine similarity, then subtract it from one.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Magnitude discards phase; a circular shift changes Fourier phase while preserving these magnitudes.
  • The DC coordinate reflects the mean, and raw magnitudes depend on record length and amplitude. Matching sampling rates and frequency grids is required.
  • This example compares unweighted nonnegative-frequency rFFT magnitudes. Comparing a full FFT, or weighting interior rFFT magnitudes by sqrt(2), gives a different distance/cosine convention.
  • Cosine ignores positive rescaling of the entire feature vector; 1-cosine is generally not a metric, and zero vectors require an explicit policy.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把序列投影到正弦、余弦基上，将实部和虚部合成为幅值，再比较频率坐标。本例保留长度 16 实数变换的全部九个非负频率坐标，包括直流分量。 余弦比较向量方向，本例特征向量均非零。

计算步骤

  1. 计算未归一化的实数 FFT，保留各频率坐标的模。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 计算余弦相似度，再用一减去该分数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 幅值表示舍弃相位；循环平移改变傅里叶相位，却保留这些幅值。
  • 直流坐标反映均值，原始幅值依赖记录长度与振幅；比较时需要一致的采样率和频率网格。
  • 本例比较未加权的非负频率 rFFT 幅值。比较完整 FFT 或将内部 rFFT 幅值乘 sqrt(2)，对应不同的距离与余弦约定。
  • 余弦忽略整个特征向量的正比例缩放；1-cosine 通常不满足度量公理，零向量还需明确处理规则。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "fft": "rfft",
  "normalization": "backward",
  "n_bins": 9,
  "include_DC": true,
  "comparison": "one_minus_cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: real-input discrete Fourier transform; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Normalized FFT-bin powers distance periodogram_distanceChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.13173288 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · periodogram_distance

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

Square the real-FFT magnitudes and normalize their sum to one. EchoTime compares these power-share vectors with Euclidean distance. The example retains all nine bins and the mean-related DC component, so every coordinate represents a share of this particular discrete power convention. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Square each rFFT magnitude, then divide by the sum over all retained nonnegative-frequency bins.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • This is EchoTime's normalized rFFT-bin convention: no detrending, window, or one-sided interior-bin doubling. It is not a physical-density PSD estimate.
  • Normalization discards total power and phase; positive amplitude rescaling alone leaves this representation unchanged.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

将实数 FFT 幅值平方，再除以总和得到功率占比向量。EchoTime 使用欧氏距离比较该向量。本例保留全部九个频点和与均值相关的直流项，因此每个坐标都是这一离散功率约定下的占比。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 对 rFFT 幅值逐点平方，再除以全部非负频点功率的总和。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 这是 EchoTime 的归一化 rFFT 频点约定：不去趋势、不加窗，也不把单边谱内部频点乘二；它不等于具有物理单位的功率谱密度估计。
  • 归一化舍弃总功率和相位；单纯的正振幅缩放不会改变此表示。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "n_coeffs": 9,
  "normalization": "divide rFFT squared magnitudes by their sum",
  "detrend": false,
  "one_sided_doubling": false,
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; NumPy: real-input discrete Fourier transform; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

PAA block means distance paa_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.61269408 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Split the 16 observations into four equal, consecutive blocks and replace each block by its mean. Comparing these ordered block averages emphasizes coarse temporal structure. The animation highlights the exact observations contributing to each coordinate. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Average each consecutive block of four observations to obtain four coordinates.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • The displayed Euclidean feature distance is unscaled; the usual length-scaled PAA lower bound additionally multiplies it by sqrt(n/w).
  • Short events within a block can cancel, and moving a block boundary can alter the representation.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把 16 个观测分成四个连续等长区间，每个区间用均值表示。比较这些有序均值可突出较粗的时间结构；动图逐块高亮每个坐标所使用的观测。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 每四个连续观测求一次均值，得到四个坐标。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 图中欧氏特征距离未经长度缩放；常用 PAA 下界还需乘以 sqrt(n/w)。
  • 区间内的短暂事件可能互相抵消，改变分段边界也会改变表示。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "n_segments": 4,
  "block_size": 4,
  "z_normalization": false,
  "distance_length_scaling": false,
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: piecewise aggregate approximation; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Full Haar coefficient distance dwt_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 1.9325587 — Full-coefficient L2; raw Euclidean distance = 1.93255872199.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d = ||W_Haar x - W_Haar y||_2 = ||x-y||_2 (complete orthonormal transform)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Apply the complete orthonormal Haar transform and compare all coefficients. The animation separates pair averages and details across four levels. With every coefficient retained and no boundary padding, the resulting Euclidean distance equals the raw-sample Euclidean distance by norm preservation.

How the calculation works

  1. Use equal-length inputs of length 16, so Haar pairs need no padding.
  2. Recursively form orthonormal pair sums and differences at four levels.
  3. Collect all 16 coefficients in [A4,D4,D3,D2,D1] order.
  4. Take their Euclidean distance and verify equality with raw Euclidean distance.

Interpretation and limits

  • Using all orthonormal coefficients does not by itself create a new Euclidean notion of similarity.
  • Truncating coefficients, weighting scales or changing boundary conventions changes this equivalence.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

进行完整正交 Haar 变换并比较全部系数。动图展示四级相邻点和差分解。保留所有系数且不填充边界时，由范数保持性质，系数的欧氏距离等于原始样本的欧氏距离。

计算步骤

  1. 使用长度均为 16 的输入，因此相邻点分组无需填充。
  2. 递归进行四级正交归一化的相邻点和差分解。
  3. 按 [A4,D4,D3,D2,D1] 顺序保留全部 16 个系数。
  4. 计算系数欧氏距离，并核对其等于原始欧氏距离。

解释与使用边界

  • 保留全部正交系数本身不会产生新的欧氏相似性定义。
  • 截断系数、对尺度加权或改变边界约定都会改变上述等价关系。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "wavelet": "orthonormal Haar",
  "length": 16,
  "retained": 16,
  "coefficient_order": "A4,D4,D3,D2,D1",
  "padding": false
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: PyWavelets: Haar approximation and detail coefficients; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Value histograms distance histogram_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.33071891 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Count observations in seven shared, fixed value intervals and divide by the sample count. The two probability vectors describe how often values occur. Shared edges make the coordinates comparable; all temporal ordering is intentionally removed. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Use identical bin edges for both inputs and normalize counts into bin probabilities.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Separate automatic binning can make coordinate-wise comparison invalid; the edges must be shared.
  • Values within one bin are indistinguishable, and permuted sequences have identical histograms.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

在七个共享、固定的数值区间中计数，再除以样本数。两个概率向量描述数值出现的频率。共享边界保证坐标可比较，同时有意舍弃全部时间顺序。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 对两条序列使用相同分箱边界，将计数归一化为概率。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 分别自动分箱可能导致坐标不可比，因此必须共享边界。
  • 同一区间内的数值无法区分，重排序列也具有相同直方图。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "edges": [
    -1.5,
    -1.0,
    -0.5,
    0.0,
    0.5,
    1.0,
    1.5,
    2.0
  ],
  "normalization": "probability mass, not density",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: histogram bins and normalization; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Permutation-entropy distance permutation_entropy_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.13023244 — Normalized entropies H(x)=0.79848069, H(y)=0.66824825

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d = |H(p)-H(q)|; H(p)=-sum(p log p)/log(3!)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Turn length-three windows into ordering patterns and compute each distribution's normalized Shannon entropy. Compare the two scalar entropies with an absolute difference. The score measures equality of this particular summary, so distinct pattern distributions can still have zero distance.

How the calculation works

  1. Form overlapping windows with order 3 and delay 1.
  2. Stably rank each window and count all six possible permutations.
  3. Compute H=-sum(p log p)/log(6) for each distribution.
  4. Take |H(x)-H(y)| and remember that entropy discards pattern identities.

Interpretation and limits

  • Order 3, delay 1 and stable tie-breaking by earlier position are explicit choices; frequent ties can change the interpretation.
  • Short sequences provide sparse pattern counts; overlapping windows are not independent samples.
  • Equal entropy does not imply equal ordinal distributions or similar dynamics.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把三点窗口转换成排序模式，分别计算模式分布的归一化 Shannon 熵，再取两标量之差的绝对值。该分数比较这一特定摘要，因而不同模式分布仍可能得到零距离。

计算步骤

  1. 使用阶数 3、延迟 1 构造重叠窗口。
  2. 稳定排序各窗口，并统计全部六种排列。
  3. 对各分布计算 H=-sum(p log p)/log(6)。
  4. 计算 |H(x)-H(y)|，同时注意熵舍弃具体模式身份。

解释与使用边界

  • 阶数 3、延迟 1，以及按较早位置优先的稳定并列处理都是明确选择；大量并列值会改变解释。
  • 短序列的模式统计稀疏，重叠窗口也不是独立样本。
  • 相同的熵不意味着相同的模式分布或动力学。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "order": 3,
  "delay": 1,
  "tie_policy": "stable by index",
  "log_base": "e",
  "entropy_normalized": true
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Bandt and Pompe (2002), permutation entropy; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions; EchoTime low-level implementation

Ordinal-pattern JS distance ordinal_pattern_js_distanceChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.25686047 — Natural-log JS distance

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · ordinal_pattern_js_distance

d = sqrt((KL(p||m)+KL(q||m))/2), m=(p+q)/2

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

Turn every three-observation window into its ordering pattern, then compare the six-pattern probability vectors with square-root Jensen-Shannon divergence. This retains which patterns are frequent and follows EchoTime's natural-log definition.

How the calculation works

  1. Form overlapping windows with order 3 and delay 1.
  2. Stably rank each window and count all six possible permutations.
  3. Compute square-root Jensen-Shannon divergence with natural logarithms.
  4. Read a distribution distance and check the chosen order/delay.

Interpretation and limits

  • Order 3, delay 1 and stable tie-breaking by earlier position are explicit choices; frequent ties can change the interpretation.
  • Short sequences provide sparse pattern counts; overlapping windows are not independent samples.
  • Pattern frequencies discard when patterns occur.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

把每个三点窗口转换成排序模式，再以 Jensen-Shannon 散度的平方根比较六种模式的概率向量。它保留具体哪些模式更常出现，并遵循 EchoTime 使用自然对数的定义。

计算步骤

  1. 使用阶数 3、延迟 1 构造重叠窗口。
  2. 稳定排序各窗口，并统计全部六种排列。
  3. 使用自然对数计算 Jensen-Shannon 散度的平方根。
  4. 读取分布距离，并检查所选阶数与延迟。

解释与使用边界

  • 阶数 3、延迟 1，以及按较早位置优先的稳定并列处理都是明确选择；大量并列值会改变解释。
  • 短序列的模式统计稀疏，重叠窗口也不是独立样本。
  • 模式频率舍弃模式出现的具体时刻。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "order": 3,
  "delay": 1,
  "tie_policy": "stable by index",
  "log_base": "e",
  "entropy_normalized": false
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Bandt and Pompe (2002), permutation entropy; SciPy: square-root Jensen-Shannon divergence; EchoTime low-level implementation

Linear trend features distance trend_distanceChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.11805668 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · trend_distance

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

Fit an ordinary least-squares line to each series on the sample-index axis. Compare the intercept, slope and population standard deviation of residuals. This is EchoTime's three-coordinate trend description; it retains overall direction and unexplained variation while compressing local structure. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Fit x_t = intercept + slope*t and append the residual population SD.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • The slope uses sample index, so changing the sampling interval changes its interpretation; physical-time comparisons require consistent units.
  • Intercept, slope and residual SD have different scales. Curved trends and different residual patterns can share the same vector.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

以样本索引为时间轴，对每条序列拟合普通最小二乘直线，再比较截距、斜率和残差的总体标准差。这是 EchoTime 的三坐标趋势描述，保留总体方向与未解释波动，同时压缩局部结构。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 拟合 x_t = 截距 + 斜率*t，并加入残差总体标准差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 斜率使用样本索引，改变采样间隔会改变其含义；按物理时间比较需要统一单位。
  • 截距、斜率与残差标准差尺度不同；弯曲趋势或不同残差结构可能共享同一向量。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "time_axis": "sample index 0..15",
  "fit": "ordinary least squares",
  "residual_std_ddof": 0,
  "features": [
    "intercept",
    "slope",
    "residual_std"
  ],
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

SAX Hamming distance sax_hamming_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative word distance: 1 — Normalized symbol mismatch rate

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d = sum_i [SAX_i(x) != SAX_i(y)] / 4

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Convert each standardized series to a four-symbol SAX word, then count the fraction of positions with different symbols. This treats every mismatch equally, including adjacent bins; it is a distance between words, with information lost during aggregation and quantization.

How the calculation works

  1. Z-normalize each complete series and use four equal PAA blocks.
  2. Quantize each mean with shared standard-normal quartile breakpoints.
  3. Count mismatching symbols.
  4. Divide the mismatch count by four.

Interpretation and limits

  • Gaussian breakpoints are a coding convention; they do not make arbitrary observed signals normally distributed.
  • Hamming on words does not have the SAX Euclidean lower-bound interpretation.
  • Both PAA aggregation and quantization can map distinct signals to the same code.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把每条标准化序列转换为四符号 SAX 单词，再统计对应位置符号不同的比例。所有不匹配均等计数，包括相邻区间；它比较符号单词，聚合和量化已经损失信息。

计算步骤

  1. 对各完整序列做 z 标准化，并计算四个等长 PAA 区间。
  2. 使用共享标准正态四分位断点量化各区间均值。
  3. 统计符号不匹配数。
  4. 将不匹配数除以四。

解释与使用边界

  • 高斯断点是一种编码约定，不会使任意观测信号变成正态分布。
  • 单词上的 Hamming 距离不具有 SAX 欧氏下界的含义。
  • PAA 聚合和量化都可能把不同信号映射为相同代码。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "length": 16,
  "word_length": 4,
  "alphabet_size": 4,
  "breakpoints": [
    -0.6744897501960817,
    0.0,
    0.6744897501960817
  ],
  "z_normalization_ddof": 0,
  "x_word": "cadb",
  "y_word": "bbcc"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Lin et al. (2007), Experiencing SAX, author copy; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

SAX MINDIST lower bound sax_mindistReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative lower bound: 0 — Compare with actual z-normalized Euclidean distance 3.3214665.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

MINDIST = sqrt(16/4)*sqrt(sum_i interval_gap(SAX_i(x),SAX_i(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Compare SAX words using the minimum gap between their symbol intervals, sum squared gaps and multiply by the PAA length factor. Equal or adjacent symbols contribute zero. In this example all paired symbols are adjacent, demonstrating why a valid lower bound can be zero for distinct series.

How the calculation works

  1. Z-normalize each complete series and use four equal PAA blocks.
  2. Quantize each mean with shared standard-normal quartile breakpoints.
  3. Look up the minimum gap between the two symbol intervals.
  4. Take sqrt(n/w)*sqrt(sum(gap^2)); this is a lower-bound calculation.

Interpretation and limits

  • Gaussian breakpoints are a coding convention; they do not make arbitrary observed signals normally distributed.
  • The Euclidean lower-bound interpretation requires the same normalized length, partitions and ordered breakpoints; lower-bounding does not imply metric axioms.
  • Both PAA aggregation and quantization can map distinct signals to the same code.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

以 SAX 符号区间之间的最小间隔比较两个单词，累加间隔平方后加入 PAA 长度系数。相同或相邻符号贡献为零。本例各对应符号都相邻，展示了有效下界为何可对不同序列返回零。

计算步骤

  1. 对各完整序列做 z 标准化，并计算四个等长 PAA 区间。
  2. 使用共享标准正态四分位断点量化各区间均值。
  3. 查找两个符号区间之间的最小间隔。
  4. 计算 sqrt(n/w)*sqrt(sum(gap^2))，得到下界。

解释与使用边界

  • 高斯断点是一种编码约定，不会使任意观测信号变成正态分布。
  • 欧氏下界解释要求相同的标准化长度、分段和有序断点；具有下界性质不等于满足度量公理。
  • PAA 聚合和量化都可能把不同信号映射为相同代码。

  "length": 16,
  "word_length": 4,
  "alphabet_size": 4,
  "breakpoints": [
    -0.6744897501960817,
    0.0,
    0.6744897501960817
  ],
  "z_normalization_ddof": 0,
  "x_word": "cadb",
  "y_word": "bbcc"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Lin et al. (2007), Experiencing SAX, author copy; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Bag-of-patterns cosine similarity bag_of_patterns_cosine_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative similarity: 0.45454545 — Explicit BoP + cosine composition; shared vocabulary.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

s = cosine(counts(SAX_windows(x)), counts(SAX_windows(y)))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Slide an eight-sample window, normalize each window, encode it as a four-letter SAX word and count the words. Consecutive duplicate words are suppressed in this example. The resulting bag is compared by cosine, so local motifs contribute through their frequencies while their global order is discarded.

How the calculation works

  1. Slide a length-eight window, normalize it, then encode four PAA means with the shared SAX alphabet.
  2. Suppress consecutive duplicate words and count both series in one shared vocabulary.
  3. Accumulate matching word-count products divided by both vector norms.
  4. Read cosine similarity between count vectors.

Interpretation and limits

  • Window length, word length, alphabet and numerosity reduction define the comparison; the original BoP representation does not mandate this exact cosine adapter.
  • Global motif order and absolute window level/scale are discarded; a similar bag need not imply aligned events.
  • A zero count vector needs an explicit policy. This example has nonempty bags.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

滑动八点窗口，对每个窗口单独标准化，编码为四字母 SAX 单词并计数。本例抑制连续重复单词，再用余弦比较词袋。局部模式通过出现频率参与比较，其全局顺序被舍弃。

计算步骤

  1. 滑动八点窗口并标准化，再用共享 SAX 字母表编码四个 PAA 均值。
  2. 抑制连续重复词，在一个共享词表中对两条序列计数。
  3. 累加对应词频的乘积，并除以两个向量的范数乘积。
  4. 读取词频向量的余弦相似度。

解释与使用边界

  • 窗口长度、词长、字母表与重复抑制规则共同定义比较；原 BoP 表示并未要求必须使用本例余弦适配器。
  • 全局模式顺序和窗口的绝对水平、尺度被舍弃；词袋相似并不意味着事件对齐。
  • 零计数向量需要明确处理规则，本例两个词袋均非空。
Example parameters / 示例参数

  "window": 8,
  "stride": 1,
  "word_length": 4,
  "alphabet_size": 4,
  "local_z_normalization": true,
  "numerosity_reduction": "consecutive exact duplicate words",
  "vocabulary": [
    "aacd",
    "abdc",
    "abdd",
    "acdb",
    "adca",
    "bacd",
    "bdda",
    "cabd",
    "cdba",
    "daac",
    "dbac",
    "dcab"
  ],
  "comparison": "cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Lin, Khade and Li (2012), bag-of-patterns representation; Lin et al. (2007), Experiencing SAX, author copy; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Bag-of-patterns cosine distance bag_of_patterns_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.54545455 — Explicit BoP + cosine composition; shared vocabulary.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d = 1 - cosine(counts(SAX_windows(x)), counts(SAX_windows(y)))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Slide an eight-sample window, normalize each window, encode it as a four-letter SAX word and count the words. Consecutive duplicate words are suppressed in this example. The resulting bag is compared by cosine, so local motifs contribute through their frequencies while their global order is discarded.

How the calculation works

  1. Slide a length-eight window, normalize it, then encode four PAA means with the shared SAX alphabet.
  2. Suppress consecutive duplicate words and count both series in one shared vocabulary.
  3. Accumulate matching word-count products divided by both vector norms.
  4. Subtract the completed cosine similarity from one.

Interpretation and limits

  • Window length, word length, alphabet and numerosity reduction define the comparison; the original BoP representation does not mandate this exact cosine adapter.
  • Global motif order and absolute window level/scale are discarded; a similar bag need not imply aligned events.
  • A zero count vector needs an explicit policy. This example has nonempty bags.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

滑动八点窗口，对每个窗口单独标准化，编码为四字母 SAX 单词并计数。本例抑制连续重复单词，再用余弦比较词袋。局部模式通过出现频率参与比较，其全局顺序被舍弃。

计算步骤

  1. 滑动八点窗口并标准化，再用共享 SAX 字母表编码四个 PAA 均值。
  2. 抑制连续重复词，在一个共享词表中对两条序列计数。
  3. 累加对应词频的乘积，并除以两个向量的范数乘积。
  4. 用一减去完整的余弦相似度。

解释与使用边界

  • 窗口长度、词长、字母表与重复抑制规则共同定义比较；原 BoP 表示并未要求必须使用本例余弦适配器。
  • 全局模式顺序和窗口的绝对水平、尺度被舍弃；词袋相似并不意味着事件对齐。
  • 零计数向量需要明确处理规则，本例两个词袋均非空。

Definition sources / 定义来源: Lin, Khade and Li (2012), bag-of-patterns representation; Lin et al. (2007), Experiencing SAX, author copy; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Independent-channel basic statistics distance independent_stats_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.42234674 — Channel distances 0.3997628 and 0.44493067; arithmetic mean.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d(X,Y) = mean_c ||phi(X[:,c])-phi(Y[:,c])||_2

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Extract basic statistics separately in each of two channels, compute one Euclidean distance per channel, then take their arithmetic mean. The displayed input concatenates channels only for visualization; the computation preserves channel boundaries and has no cross-channel dependence term.

How the calculation works

  1. Keep corresponding channel identities and units aligned.
  2. Extract basic statistics independently from each channel.
  3. Compute the Euclidean feature distance inside each channel.
  4. Average the two channel distances with equal weights.

Interpretation and limits

  • Permuting observations leaves this vector unchanged, even when temporal behavior changes completely.
  • Features have different scales; this example uses no coordinate weighting. A production scaler must be fitted without test-data leakage.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.
  • Averaging independent channel distances cannot detect cross-channel timing or dependence changes that preserve each marginal descriptor.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

分别从两个通道提取基础统计量，各自计算一个欧氏距离，再取算术平均。图中的输入仅为展示而按通道拼接；计算始终保留通道边界，不包含跨通道依赖项。

计算步骤

  1. 保持对应通道身份与单位一致。
  2. 逐通道独立提取基础统计量。
  3. 分别计算每个通道内部的欧氏特征距离。
  4. 对两个通道距离等权平均。

解释与使用边界

  • 任意重排序列不会改变该向量，即使时间行为发生显著变化。
  • 各坐标尺度不同；本例不加权。实际使用的缩放器应避免测试数据泄漏。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
  • 独立通道距离的平均无法识别保持各通道边缘描述不变的跨通道时序或依赖变化。

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: summary-statistic definitions; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Independent-channel paa block means distance independent_paa_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.47351384 — Channel distances 0.61269408 and 0.33433359; arithmetic mean.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d(X,Y) = mean_c ||phi(X[:,c])-phi(Y[:,c])||_2

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Extract paa block means separately in each of two channels, compute one Euclidean distance per channel, then take their arithmetic mean. The displayed input concatenates channels only for visualization; the computation preserves channel boundaries and has no cross-channel dependence term.

How the calculation works

  1. Keep corresponding channel identities and units aligned.
  2. Extract paa block means independently from each channel.
  3. Compute the Euclidean feature distance inside each channel.
  4. Average the two channel distances with equal weights.

Interpretation and limits

  • The displayed Euclidean feature distance is unscaled; the usual length-scaled PAA lower bound additionally multiplies it by sqrt(n/w).
  • Short events within a block can cancel, and moving a block boundary can alter the representation.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.
  • Averaging independent channel distances cannot detect cross-channel timing or dependence changes that preserve each marginal descriptor.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

分别从两个通道提取PAA 分段均值，各自计算一个欧氏距离，再取算术平均。图中的输入仅为展示而按通道拼接；计算始终保留通道边界，不包含跨通道依赖项。

计算步骤

  1. 保持对应通道身份与单位一致。
  2. 逐通道独立提取PAA 分段均值。
  3. 分别计算每个通道内部的欧氏特征距离。
  4. 对两个通道距离等权平均。

解释与使用边界

  • 图中欧氏特征距离未经长度缩放；常用 PAA 下界还需乘以 sqrt(n/w)。
  • 区间内的短暂事件可能互相抵消，改变分段边界也会改变表示。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
  • 独立通道距离的平均无法识别保持各通道边缘描述不变的跨通道时序或依赖变化。

Fitted dynamics and models / 动力学与模型 5
Autoregressive-coefficient distance ar_distanceSpecified illustrative protocol

Illustrative distance: 0.33905042 — Distance between separately demeaned conditional-OLS AR(2) coefficients.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

z_t = phi_1*z_(t-1)+phi_2*z_(t-2)+e_t; d=||phi_x-phi_y||_2

Demonstration: Specified illustrative protocol

Fit a separately centered AR(2) model by conditional least squares to each short input, then compare the two lag coefficients. The distance describes this specified fitted linear recurrence. Mean level and innovation variance are excluded from the coefficient vector in this example.

How the calculation works

  1. Remove each series' sample mean and construct two lagged predictors.
  2. Fit the same AR(2) equation by ordinary least squares for each input.
  3. Compare the corresponding lag-one and lag-two coefficients.
  4. Take the Euclidean norm of the coefficient difference.

Interpretation and limits

  • The example fixes order two and demeans each series separately. Changing order, centering or estimator changes the fitted object.
  • Sixteen samples suffice only for an illustration, not a reliable claim about the generating process.
  • Coefficient similarity does not establish equal innovation noise, nonlinear dependence or causal dynamics.

示例性质: 明确设定的流程示例

分别对两条短序列去均值，用条件最小二乘拟合 AR(2)，再比较两个滞后系数。距离描述这一明确指定的线性递推拟合；本例系数向量不包含均值水平和创新方差。

计算步骤

  1. 去除各序列样本均值，构造两个滞后预测量。
  2. 分别用普通最小二乘拟合相同形式的 AR(2) 方程。
  3. 比较对应的一阶和二阶滞后系数。
  4. 计算系数差的欧氏范数。

解释与使用边界

  • 本例固定二阶，并分别去均值；改变阶数、中心化或估计器都会改变拟合对象。
  • 十六个样本仅用于解释过程，不能据此可靠推断生成机制。
  • 系数相似不能证明创新噪声、非线性依赖或因果动力学相同。

  "demean": "separately using full series mean",
  "intercept": false,
  "estimator": "conditional OLS, no regularization",
  "residual_variance": "mean squared fitted residual",
  "beta_x": [
    1.0243090422834804,
    -0.6188171023843692
  ],

    0.7866492880466931

Definition sources / 定义来源: statsmodels: conditional least-squares autoregression; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

AR log-spectrum distance ar_spectrum_distanceSpecified illustrative protocol

Illustrative distance: 5.9653596 — Unweighted L2 over 17 natural-log spectral coordinates; no integration weighting.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

S(w)=sigma^2/|1-sum_(j=1)^2 phi_j exp(-ijw)|^2; d=||log S_x-log S_y||_2

Demonstration: Specified illustrative protocol

Fit the same centered AR(2) model to each input and evaluate its implied spectrum on 17 shared frequencies. The animation compares natural-log spectra with Euclidean distance. The two final fits are stable; their fitted residual variances are included, so both spectral shape and power level affect the score.

How the calculation works

  1. Demean each input, build two lags and fit conditional-OLS AR(2) models.
  2. Check stability and evaluate sigma^2 / |1-phi_1 exp(-iw)-phi_2 exp(-2iw)|^2.
  3. Take natural logs on the same 17-point frequency grid.
  4. Take the Euclidean norm of the log-spectrum difference.

Interpretation and limits

  • The example fixes order two and demeans each series separately. Changing order, centering or estimator changes the fitted object.
  • Sixteen samples suffice only for an illustration, not a reliable claim about the generating process.
  • The spectrum is an AR model implication; stability and stationarity assumptions require scrutiny. Grid density and log conventions change the numeric distance.

示例性质: 明确设定的流程示例

对两条输入拟合相同的中心化 AR(2) 模型，并在 17 个共享频点上计算模型隐含谱。动图以欧氏距离比较自然对数谱。两个最终拟合均稳定，且纳入拟合残差方差，因此谱形状和功率水平都会影响分数。

计算步骤

  1. 分别去均值，构造两个滞后量并拟合条件最小二乘 AR(2)。
  2. 检查稳定性，计算 sigma^2 / |1-phi_1 exp(-iw)-phi_2 exp(-2iw)|^2。
  3. 在相同的 17 点频率网格上取自然对数。
  4. 计算对数谱差的欧氏范数。

解释与使用边界

  • 本例固定二阶，并分别去均值；改变阶数、中心化或估计器都会改变拟合对象。
  • 十六个样本仅用于解释过程，不能据此可靠推断生成机制。
  • 该谱是 AR 模型的推论，需要检查稳定性和平稳性假设。网格密度及对数约定会改变数值距离。

Symmetric AR cross-prediction error ar_prediction_error_distanceSpecified illustrative protocol

Illustrative symmetric cross error: 0.097047155 — MSE(x model -> y)=0.047154603; MSE(y model -> x)=0.14693971.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

d = (mean((y_centered-A_y phi_x)^2)+mean((x_centered-A_x phi_y)^2))/2

Demonstration: Specified illustrative protocol

Fit centered AR(2) models, apply the x model to y and the y model to x, then average their one-step mean-squared errors. This measures compatibility under a specified predictive model and centering convention. Even a self-comparison can remain positive because fitted innovations are not zero.

How the calculation works

  1. Fit one centered conditional-OLS AR(2) model to each full input.
  2. Transfer the x coefficients to centered y, and transfer the y coefficients to centered x.
  3. Compute each direction's one-step squared residuals on the available 14 lagged targets.
  4. Average the two directional MSEs; interpret this as error rather than a mathematical metric.

Interpretation and limits

  • The example fixes order two and demeans each series separately. Changing order, centering or estimator changes the fitted object.
  • Sixteen samples suffice only for an illustration, not a reliable claim about the generating process.
  • This is not a held-out forecast evaluation: both models are fitted to the displayed records and only the comparison mechanism is illustrated. Self-error need not be zero.

示例性质: 明确设定的流程示例

拟合中心化 AR(2) 模型，用 x 的模型预测 y，再用 y 的模型预测 x，最后平均两个一步预测均方误差。它度量指定预测模型和中心化约定下的兼容性；即使自比较，也可能因拟合创新不为零而得到正值。

计算步骤

  1. 分别对完整输入拟合中心化条件最小二乘 AR(2)。
  2. 将 x 的系数用于中心化 y，再将 y 的系数用于中心化 x。
  3. 对各方向的 14 个可用滞后目标计算一步预测残差平方。
  4. 平均两个方向的 MSE，把结果解释为误差分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 本例固定二阶，并分别去均值；改变阶数、中心化或估计器都会改变拟合对象。
  • 十六个样本仅用于解释过程，不能据此可靠推断生成机制。
  • 这里并非留出预测评估：模型拟合于展示的记录，仅用于解释比较机制；自比较误差不必为零。

Definition sources / 定义来源: statsmodels: conditional least-squares autoregression; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Discretized Markov-transition distance markov_distanceSpecified illustrative protocol

Illustrative distance: 0.18856181 — Frobenius norm on conditional transition probabilities; rows unweighted.

EchoTime coverage: Possible addition

P_ij = count(s_t=i,s_(t+1)=j) / count(s_t=i); d=||P_x-P_y||_F

Demonstration: Specified illustrative protocol

Map observations to three shared value states and count consecutive state transitions. Row-normalized count matrices describe the next-state distribution conditional on the current state. Compare these matrices by Frobenius distance. This illustrates a specified first-order discretization, without establishing that either signal is generated by a Markov chain.

How the calculation works

  1. Use shared thresholds 0 and 0.7, then count consecutive transitions between the three states.
  2. Divide each row by its outgoing-transition count; every row is observed in this example.
  3. Square differences between corresponding conditional transition probabilities.
  4. Sum these squared differences and take their square root, giving the Frobenius norm.

Interpretation and limits

  • State boundaries and sampling interval define the estimated transitions; independently chosen bins would not have matching meanings.
  • Unobserved rows need a documented smoothing or missing-row policy. No smoothing is used here because every row has observations.
  • First-order transition matrices discard longer history and do not prove causal equivalence or equal state occupancy.

示例性质: 明确设定的流程示例

把观测映射到三个共享数值状态，统计连续状态转移。按行归一化的计数矩阵描述给定当前状态时的下一状态分布，再以 Frobenius 距离比较两个矩阵。这只是指定的一阶离散化示例，并未证明信号由 Markov 链生成。

计算步骤

  1. 用共享阈值 0 和 0.7 定义三个状态，再统计连续转移。
  2. 用各行转出总数进行归一化；本例每行均有观测。
  3. 对相对应的条件转移概率计算差的平方。
  4. 累加这些平方差并开平方，得到 Frobenius 范数。

解释与使用边界

  • 状态边界和采样间隔决定转移估计；分别选取的分箱可能不具有对应含义。
  • 未观测行需要明确平滑或缺失规则。本例每行均有观测，因此不平滑。
  • 一阶转移矩阵舍弃更长历史，也不能证明因果等价或状态占比相同。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "state_thresholds": [
    0.0,
    0.7
  ],
  "order": 1,
  "smoothing": 0,
  "unobserved_rows": "not present in this example",
  "P_x": [
    [
      0.5,
      0.5,
      0.0
    ],
    [
      0.4,
      0.2,
      0.4
    ],
    [
      0.0,
      0.3333333333333333,
      0.6666666666666666
    ]
  ],
  "P_y": [
    [
      0.5,
      0.5,
      0.0
    ],
    [
      0.3333333333333333,
      0.3333333333333333,
      0.3333333333333333
    ],
    [
      0.0,
      0.4,
      0.6
    ]
  ]
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Linear-trend model distance linear_trend_model_distanceChecked against EchoTime

Illustrative distance: 0.11805668 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Implemented in EchoTime · linear_trend_model_distance

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Checked against EchoTime

Fit an ordinary least-squares line to each series on the sample-index axis. Compare the intercept, slope and population standard deviation of residuals. This is EchoTime's three-coordinate trend description; it retains overall direction and unexplained variation while compressing local structure. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Fit x_t = intercept + slope*t and append the residual population SD.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • The slope uses sample index, so changing the sampling interval changes its interpretation; physical-time comparisons require consistent units.
  • Intercept, slope and residual SD have different scales. Curved trends and different residual patterns can share the same vector.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 已与 EchoTime 计算核对

以样本索引为时间轴，对每条序列拟合普通最小二乘直线，再比较截距、斜率和残差的总体标准差。这是 EchoTime 的三坐标趋势描述，保留总体方向与未解释波动，同时压缩局部结构。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 拟合 x_t = 截距 + 斜率*t，并加入残差总体标准差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 斜率使用样本索引，改变采样间隔会改变其含义；按物理时间比较需要统一单位。
  • 截距、斜率与残差标准差尺度不同；弯曲趋势或不同残差结构可能共享同一向量。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "time_axis": "sample index 0..15",
  "fit": "ordinary least squares",
  "residual_std_ddof": 0,
  "features": [
    "intercept",
    "slope",
    "residual_std"
  ],
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Representation and comparison / 表示与比较 24
Specified encoder protocol embedding distance embedding_distanceSpecified illustrative protocol

Illustrative distance: 0.082672381 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Specified illustrative protocol

An embedding comparison first specifies an encoder, then a distance or similarity on its output. This diagram instantiates that protocol with the fixed vector [mean, population SD, adjusted lag-one ACF, linear slope]. It demonstrates the interface with actual numbers; no learned or pretrained representation is used. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Apply the explicitly specified four-statistic encoder identically to both inputs.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • This name is an interface pattern, not one uniquely defined algorithm; another encoder can produce entirely different neighbors.
  • The illustrative coordinates have different units and receive no scaling. No semantic, causal or task-transfer guarantee follows from a small score.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 明确设定的流程示例

嵌入比较先指定编码器，再指定输出空间的距离或相似度。本图使用固定的 [均值、总体标准差、调整后一阶自相关、线性斜率] 向量，以真实计算演示接口；未使用学习得到的表示或预训练模型。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 对两条输入使用完全相同、明确指定的四统计量编码器。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 该名称描述接口模式，不对应唯一算法；换用其他编码器会改变近邻关系。
  • 示例坐标单位不同且未缩放；较小分数不蕴含语义、因果或任务迁移保证。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。

    "mean",
    "std_ddof0",
    "adjusted_ACF_lag1",
    "OLS_slope"
  ],
  "learned_weights": false,
  "coordinate_scaling": "none",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions

Specified encoder protocol embedding similarity embedding_similaritySpecified illustrative protocol

Illustrative similarity: 0.99646416 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

s(x,y) = dot(phi(x),phi(y)) / (norm(phi(x))*norm(phi(y)))

Demonstration: Specified illustrative protocol

An embedding comparison first specifies an encoder, then a distance or similarity on its output. This diagram instantiates that protocol with the fixed vector [mean, population SD, adjusted lag-one ACF, linear slope]. It demonstrates the interface with actual numbers; no learned or pretrained representation is used. Cosine compares vector direction and is defined here for nonzero feature vectors.

How the calculation works

  1. Apply the explicitly specified four-statistic encoder identically to both inputs.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Normalize by both vector lengths and sum coordinate products to obtain cosine similarity.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • This name is an interface pattern, not one uniquely defined algorithm; another encoder can produce entirely different neighbors.
  • The illustrative coordinates have different units and receive no scaling. No semantic, causal or task-transfer guarantee follows from a small score.
  • Cosine ignores positive rescaling of the entire feature vector; 1-cosine is generally not a metric, and zero vectors require an explicit policy.

示例性质: 明确设定的流程示例

嵌入比较先指定编码器，再指定输出空间的距离或相似度。本图使用固定的 [均值、总体标准差、调整后一阶自相关、线性斜率] 向量，以真实计算演示接口；未使用学习得到的表示或预训练模型。 余弦比较向量方向，本例特征向量均非零。

计算步骤

  1. 对两条输入使用完全相同、明确指定的四统计量编码器。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 用两个向量的长度归一化坐标乘积之和，得到余弦相似度。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 该名称描述接口模式，不对应唯一算法；换用其他编码器会改变近邻关系。
  • 示例坐标单位不同且未缩放；较小分数不蕴含语义、因果或任务迁移保证。
  • 余弦忽略整个特征向量的正比例缩放；1-cosine 通常不满足度量公理，零向量还需明确处理规则。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "encoder": [
    "mean",
    "std_ddof0",
    "adjusted_ACF_lag1",
    "OLS_slope"
  ],
  "learned_weights": false,
  "coordinate_scaling": "none",
  "comparison": "cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions

PAA block means embedding distance paa_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.61269408 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Split the 16 observations into four equal, consecutive blocks and replace each block by its mean. Comparing these ordered block averages emphasizes coarse temporal structure. The animation highlights the exact observations contributing to each coordinate. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Average each consecutive block of four observations to obtain four coordinates.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • The displayed Euclidean feature distance is unscaled; the usual length-scaled PAA lower bound additionally multiplies it by sqrt(n/w).
  • Short events within a block can cancel, and moving a block boundary can alter the representation.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把 16 个观测分成四个连续等长区间，每个区间用均值表示。比较这些有序均值可突出较粗的时间结构；动图逐块高亮每个坐标所使用的观测。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 每四个连续观测求一次均值，得到四个坐标。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 图中欧氏特征距离未经长度缩放；常用 PAA 下界还需乘以 sqrt(n/w)。
  • 区间内的短暂事件可能互相抵消，改变分段边界也会改变表示。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "n_segments": 4,
  "block_size": 4,
  "z_normalization": false,
  "distance_length_scaling": false,
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: tslearn: piecewise aggregate approximation; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

DFT magnitudes embedding distance dft_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 1.9042105 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Project the series onto sine and cosine bases, combine the real and imaginary parts into magnitudes, and compare the resulting frequency bins. The example retains all nine nonnegative-frequency bins of a length-16 real transform, including the DC bin. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Compute the unnormalized real FFT and retain the magnitude of each bin.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Magnitude discards phase; a circular shift changes Fourier phase while preserving these magnitudes.
  • The DC coordinate reflects the mean, and raw magnitudes depend on record length and amplitude. Matching sampling rates and frequency grids is required.
  • This example compares unweighted nonnegative-frequency rFFT magnitudes. Comparing a full FFT, or weighting interior rFFT magnitudes by sqrt(2), gives a different distance/cosine convention.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把序列投影到正弦、余弦基上，将实部和虚部合成为幅值，再比较频率坐标。本例保留长度 16 实数变换的全部九个非负频率坐标，包括直流分量。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 计算未归一化的实数 FFT，保留各频率坐标的模。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 幅值表示舍弃相位；循环平移改变傅里叶相位，却保留这些幅值。
  • 直流坐标反映均值，原始幅值依赖记录长度与振幅；比较时需要一致的采样率和频率网格。
  • 本例比较未加权的非负频率 rFFT 幅值。比较完整 FFT 或将内部 rFFT 幅值乘 sqrt(2)，对应不同的距离与余弦约定。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "fft": "rfft",
  "normalization": "backward",
  "n_bins": 9,
  "include_DC": true,
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: real-input discrete Fourier transform; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: real-input discrete Fourier transform; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Basic statistics embedding distance stats_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.3997628 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Represent each series by its mean, population standard deviation, minimum and maximum. These four coordinates summarize level, spread and range; their order in the vector is fixed, while the original temporal order is discarded. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Compute mean, population SD, minimum and maximum in that order.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Permuting observations leaves this vector unchanged, even when temporal behavior changes completely.
  • Features have different scales; this example uses no coordinate weighting. A production scaler must be fitted without test-data leakage.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

把每条序列表示为均值、总体标准差、最小值和最大值四个坐标。它们概括水平、波动和范围，坐标顺序固定，但原始时间顺序被舍弃。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 按均值、总体标准差、最小值、最大值的顺序提取特征。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 任意重排序列不会改变该向量，即使时间行为发生显著变化。
  • 各坐标尺度不同；本例不加权。实际使用的缩放器应避免测试数据泄漏。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "features": [
    "mean",
    "std_ddof0",
    "min",
    "max"
  ],
  "coordinate_scaling": "none",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: summary-statistic definitions; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: summary-statistic definitions; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Robust statistics embedding distance robust_stats_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.12641944 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Replace extrema and moments with the lower quartile, median, upper quartile and median absolute deviation. This explicit descriptor emphasizes the central distribution and reduces the influence of isolated extreme values. It does not preserve the timing of those values. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Calculate Q25, median, Q75 and unscaled median absolute deviation.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Robustness concerns these particular statistics, not immunity to arbitrary contamination or distribution shift.
  • Quantiles use linear interpolation and MAD is unscaled; alternative conventions produce different vectors.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

使用下四分位数、中位数、上四分位数和中位绝对偏差构成明确的描述向量。它强调分布的中心部分，降低孤立极端值的影响，同时舍弃这些数值出现的时刻。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 计算 Q25、中位数、Q75 和未经常数缩放的中位绝对偏差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 稳健性针对所选统计量，并不表示可抵抗任意污染或分布变化。
  • 本例分位数采用线性插值，MAD 不乘缩放常数；其他约定会产生不同向量。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "features": [
    "q25",
    "median",
    "q75",
    "unscaled_MAD"
  ],
  "quantile_method": "linear",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: summary-statistic definitions; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Robust statistics embedding similarity robust_stats_embedding_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative similarity: 0.9949377 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

s(x,y) = dot(phi(x),phi(y)) / (norm(phi(x))*norm(phi(y)))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Replace extrema and moments with the lower quartile, median, upper quartile and median absolute deviation. This explicit descriptor emphasizes the central distribution and reduces the influence of isolated extreme values. It does not preserve the timing of those values. Cosine compares vector direction and is defined here for nonzero feature vectors.

How the calculation works

  1. Calculate Q25, median, Q75 and unscaled median absolute deviation.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Normalize by both vector lengths and sum coordinate products to obtain cosine similarity.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Robustness concerns these particular statistics, not immunity to arbitrary contamination or distribution shift.
  • Quantiles use linear interpolation and MAD is unscaled; alternative conventions produce different vectors.
  • Cosine ignores positive rescaling of the entire feature vector; 1-cosine is generally not a metric, and zero vectors require an explicit policy.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

使用下四分位数、中位数、上四分位数和中位绝对偏差构成明确的描述向量。它强调分布的中心部分，降低孤立极端值的影响，同时舍弃这些数值出现的时刻。 余弦比较向量方向，本例特征向量均非零。

计算步骤

  1. 计算 Q25、中位数、Q75 和未经常数缩放的中位绝对偏差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 用两个向量的长度归一化坐标乘积之和，得到余弦相似度。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 稳健性针对所选统计量，并不表示可抵抗任意污染或分布变化。
  • 本例分位数采用线性插值，MAD 不乘缩放常数；其他约定会产生不同向量。
  • 余弦忽略整个特征向量的正比例缩放；1-cosine 通常不满足度量公理，零向量还需明确处理规则。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "features": [
    "q25",
    "median",
    "q75",
    "unscaled_MAD"
  ],
  "quantile_method": "linear",
  "comparison": "cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: summary-statistic definitions; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Autocorrelation features embedding distance acf_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.36880413 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Center each series and compare it with delayed versions of itself. Six lag coordinates summarize repeated linear dependence. This example follows EchoTime's adjusted denominator, (n-lag) times the population variance, and omits the always-one lag-zero coordinate. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. At each lag, sum centered lagged products and divide by (n-lag) times variance.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Adjusted high-lag sample estimates need not remain inside [-1,1]; estimates are less reliable with fewer overlapping pairs.
  • ACF drops the mean and amplitude scale and cannot fully identify nonlinear dependence or the original waveform.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

先对序列去均值，再与自身的延迟版本比较。六个滞后坐标描述重复出现的线性依赖。本例采用 EchoTime 的调整分母，即 (n-lag) 乘总体方差，并省略恒为一的零滞后坐标。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 逐个滞后累加去均值后的乘积，除以 (n-lag) 乘方差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 经过样本数调整的高滞后估计不一定落在 [-1,1]，重叠点越少估计越不稳定。
  • ACF 舍弃均值和振幅尺度，无法完整识别非线性依赖或恢复原波形。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "max_lag": 6,
  "denominator": "(n-lag)*population_variance",
  "lag_zero": "omitted",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; statsmodels: ACF denominator and adjusted convention; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Coarse Haar coefficients embedding distance wavelet_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 1.586823 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Recursively average and difference adjacent pairs with orthonormal Haar scaling. Keep the coarsest average and the next three detail levels: eight of the 16 coefficients. Comparing this vector emphasizes retained scales while deliberately discarding the finest eight detail coefficients. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Apply Haar pair averages/differences recursively and retain [A4,D4,D3,D2].
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • This is a truncated transform; different high-frequency details can produce exactly the same retained vector.
  • Haar coefficients depend on alignment and boundary handling. Here the length is a power of two and no padding is needed.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

对相邻点反复计算带正交归一化的和与差。本例保留最粗近似及随后三级细节，共 16 个系数中的八个；比较这些坐标强调所保留尺度，并主动舍弃最细的八个细节系数。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 递归计算 Haar 相邻点和差，保留 [A4,D4,D3,D2]。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 这里使用截断变换；不同的高频细节可能产生完全相同的保留向量。
  • Haar 系数依赖对齐与边界处理。本例长度为二的幂，因此无需填充。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "wavelet": "orthonormal Haar",
  "length": 16,
  "coefficient_order": "A4,D4,D3,D2,D1",
  "retained": 8,
  "boundary": "no padding",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: PyWavelets: Haar approximation and detail coefficients; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Definition sources / 定义来源: PyWavelets: Haar approximation and detail coefficients; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Value histograms embedding distance histogram_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.33071891 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Count observations in seven shared, fixed value intervals and divide by the sample count. The two probability vectors describe how often values occur. Shared edges make the coordinates comparable; all temporal ordering is intentionally removed. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Use identical bin edges for both inputs and normalize counts into bin probabilities.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Separate automatic binning can make coordinate-wise comparison invalid; the edges must be shared.
  • Values within one bin are indistinguishable, and permuted sequences have identical histograms.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

在七个共享、固定的数值区间中计数，再除以样本数。两个概率向量描述数值出现的频率。共享边界保证坐标可比较，同时有意舍弃全部时间顺序。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 对两条序列使用相同分箱边界，将计数归一化为概率。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 分别自动分箱可能导致坐标不可比，因此必须共享边界。
  • 同一区间内的数值无法区分，重排序列也具有相同直方图。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "edges": [
    -1.5,
    -1.0,
    -0.5,
    0.0,
    0.5,
    1.0,
    1.5,
    2.0
  ],
  "normalization": "probability mass, not density",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: histogram bins and normalization; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Definition sources / 定义来源: NumPy: histogram bins and normalization; Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Normalized FFT-bin powers embedding distance periodogram_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.13173288 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Square the real-FFT magnitudes and normalize their sum to one. EchoTime compares these power-share vectors with Euclidean distance. The example retains all nine bins and the mean-related DC component, so every coordinate represents a share of this particular discrete power convention. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Square each rFFT magnitude, then divide by the sum over all retained nonnegative-frequency bins.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • This is EchoTime's normalized rFFT-bin convention: no detrending, window, or one-sided interior-bin doubling. It is not a physical-density PSD estimate.
  • Normalization discards total power and phase; positive amplitude rescaling alone leaves this representation unchanged.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

将实数 FFT 幅值平方，再除以总和得到功率占比向量。EchoTime 使用欧氏距离比较该向量。本例保留全部九个频点和与均值相关的直流项，因此每个坐标都是这一离散功率约定下的占比。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 对 rFFT 幅值逐点平方，再除以全部非负频点功率的总和。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 这是 EchoTime 的归一化 rFFT 频点约定：不去趋势、不加窗，也不把单边谱内部频点乘二；它不等于具有物理单位的功率谱密度估计。
  • 归一化舍弃总功率和相位；单纯的正振幅缩放不会改变此表示。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "n_coeffs": 9,
  "normalization": "divide rFFT squared magnitudes by their sum",
  "detrend": false,
  "one_sided_doubling": false,
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; NumPy: real-input discrete Fourier transform; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

解释与使用边界

  • 这是 EchoTime 的归一化 rFFT 频点约定：不去趋势、不加窗，也不把单边谱内部频点乘二；它不等于具有物理单位的功率谱密度估计。
  • 归一化舍弃总功率和相位；单纯的正振幅缩放不会改变此表示。
  • 余弦忽略整个特征向量的正比例缩放；1-cosine 通常不满足度量公理，零向量还需明确处理规则。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "n_coeffs": 9,
  "normalization": "divide rFFT squared magnitudes by their sum",
  "detrend": false,
  "one_sided_doubling": false,
  "comparison": "cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; NumPy: real-input discrete Fourier transform; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Linear trend features embedding distance trend_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.11805668 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Fit an ordinary least-squares line to each series on the sample-index axis. Compare the intercept, slope and population standard deviation of residuals. This is EchoTime's three-coordinate trend description; it retains overall direction and unexplained variation while compressing local structure. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Fit x_t = intercept + slope*t and append the residual population SD.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • The slope uses sample index, so changing the sampling interval changes its interpretation; physical-time comparisons require consistent units.
  • Intercept, slope and residual SD have different scales. Curved trends and different residual patterns can share the same vector.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

以样本索引为时间轴，对每条序列拟合普通最小二乘直线，再比较截距、斜率和残差的总体标准差。这是 EchoTime 的三坐标趋势描述，保留总体方向与未解释波动，同时压缩局部结构。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 拟合 x_t = 截距 + 斜率*t，并加入残差总体标准差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 斜率使用样本索引，改变采样间隔会改变其含义；按物理时间比较需要统一单位。
  • 截距、斜率与残差标准差尺度不同；弯曲趋势或不同残差结构可能共享同一向量。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "time_axis": "sample index 0..15",
  "fit": "ordinary least squares",
  "residual_std_ddof": 0,
  "features": [
    "intercept",
    "slope",
    "residual_std"
  ],
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Linear trend features embedding similarity trend_embedding_similarityReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative similarity: 0.99811962 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

s(x,y) = dot(phi(x),phi(y)) / (norm(phi(x))*norm(phi(y)))

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Fit an ordinary least-squares line to each series on the sample-index axis. Compare the intercept, slope and population standard deviation of residuals. This is EchoTime's three-coordinate trend description; it retains overall direction and unexplained variation while compressing local structure. Cosine compares vector direction and is defined here for nonzero feature vectors.

How the calculation works

  1. Fit x_t = intercept + slope*t and append the residual population SD.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Normalize by both vector lengths and sum coordinate products to obtain cosine similarity.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • The slope uses sample index, so changing the sampling interval changes its interpretation; physical-time comparisons require consistent units.
  • Intercept, slope and residual SD have different scales. Curved trends and different residual patterns can share the same vector.
  • Cosine ignores positive rescaling of the entire feature vector; 1-cosine is generally not a metric, and zero vectors require an explicit policy.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

以样本索引为时间轴，对每条序列拟合普通最小二乘直线，再比较截距、斜率和残差的总体标准差。这是 EchoTime 的三坐标趋势描述，保留总体方向与未解释波动，同时压缩局部结构。 余弦比较向量方向，本例特征向量均非零。

计算步骤

  1. 拟合 x_t = 截距 + 斜率*t，并加入残差总体标准差。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 用两个向量的长度归一化坐标乘积之和，得到余弦相似度。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 斜率使用样本索引，改变采样间隔会改变其含义；按物理时间比较需要统一单位。
  • 截距、斜率与残差标准差尺度不同；弯曲趋势或不同残差结构可能共享同一向量。
  • 余弦忽略整个特征向量的正比例缩放；1-cosine 通常不满足度量公理，零向量还需明确处理规则。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "time_axis": "sample index 0..15",
  "fit": "ordinary least squares",
  "residual_std_ddof": 0,
  "features": [
    "intercept",
    "slope",
    "residual_std"
  ],
  "comparison": "cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: EchoTime low-level implementation; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Fixed Gaussian projection embedding distance random_projection_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 2.1747811 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Multiply both 16-coordinate inputs by the same six-by-16 Gaussian matrix. Each output coordinate is a weighted sum of all observations. The matrix is fixed by an explicit random seed, allowing the comparison to be reproduced without fitting a neural encoder. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Use the same fixed matrix with entries N(0,1/6) to project both inputs to six dimensions.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • The six-dimensional example has no asserted distortion guarantee. Johnson-Lindenstrauss guarantees require dimensions and probability assumptions tied to a finite point set.
  • Changing the random seed changes the representation; a shared matrix and identical input length/order are essential.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

将两个 16 维输入乘以同一个六乘十六高斯矩阵。每个输出坐标都是全部观测的加权和。矩阵由明确的随机种子固定，因此无需拟合神经编码器也可复现比较。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 使用同一个元素分布为 N(0,1/6) 的固定矩阵，把两条输入投影到六维。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 本例六维投影不声称满足给定失真保证。Johnson-Lindenstrauss 保证需要维数、概率与有限样本集等条件。
  • 改变随机种子会改变表示；必须共享矩阵并保持输入长度及顺序一致。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。

Definition sources / 定义来源: scikit-learn: Gaussian random projection; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Identity-resample features embedding distance identity_embedding_distanceReference-formula demonstration

Illustrative distance: 0.9749464 — Computed from the explicitly defined illustrative encoder; no learned weights.

EchoTime coverage: Low-priority addition

d(x,y) = sqrt(sum_j (phi_j(x)-phi_j(y))^2)

Demonstration: Reference-formula demonstration

Linearly interpolate each series onto the same eight-point normalized-time grid and use the samples themselves as coordinates. This explicit identity-resample adapter aligns the representation length without learning a latent model or aligning local events. Euclidean distance retains differences in both vector scale and coordinates.

How the calculation works

  1. Map each span to [0,1] and linearly interpolate at eight shared grid positions.
  2. Collect the computed coordinates in the same fixed order for both inputs.
  3. Take the Euclidean norm of the coordinate differences.
  4. Interpret the score in this representation, accounting for the information it discards.

Interpretation and limits

  • Normalizing time spans discards absolute duration unless it is retained separately.
  • Interpolation can miss or smooth brief events; it provides no invariance to arbitrary local timing changes.
  • A metric on these feature vectors can be only a pseudometric on the original series when the representation loses information.

示例性质: 参考公式演示

将每条序列线性插值到同一个八点归一化时间网格，直接把重采样值作为坐标。这个明确的恒等重采样适配器统一表示长度，无需学习潜在模型，也不进行局部事件对齐。 欧氏距离同时保留向量尺度与坐标差异。

计算步骤

  1. 把各自时间跨度映射到 [0,1]，在八个共享位置进行线性插值。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 对坐标差计算欧氏范数。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 分别归一化时间跨度会舍弃绝对持续时间，除非另行保留。
  • 插值可能遗漏或平滑短暂事件，也不提供任意局部时间变化下的不变性。
  • 当表示损失信息时，特征空间中的度量在原序列空间上可能只是伪度量。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "n_points": 8,
  "interpolation": "linear",
  "grid": "independent normalized spans [0,1]",
  "comparison": "euclidean"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; NumPy: one-dimensional linear interpolation; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

计算步骤

  1. 把各自时间跨度映射到 [0,1]，在八个共享位置进行线性插值。
  2. 把计算得到的坐标按同一个固定顺序组成两个向量。
  3. 用两个向量的长度归一化坐标乘积之和，得到余弦相似度。
  4. 结合表示所舍弃的信息，在该表示空间中解释分数。

解释与使用边界

  • 分别归一化时间跨度会舍弃绝对持续时间，除非另行保留。
  • 插值可能遗漏或平滑短暂事件，也不提供任意局部时间变化下的不变性。
  • 余弦忽略整个特征向量的正比例缩放；1-cosine 通常不满足度量公理，零向量还需明确处理规则。
Example parameters / 示例参数
{
  "n_points": 8,
  "interpolation": "linear",
  "grid": "independent normalized spans [0,1]",
  "comparison": "cosine"
}

Definition sources / 定义来源: Imported registry: named feature and comparison conventions; NumPy: one-dimensional linear interpolation; Exact illustrative inputs and encoder definitions

Operating modes for elastic methods
  • `mode="exact"` keeps the full elastic score path and stays the default when you need a final value for a report, notebook, or paper.
  • `mode="fast"` is the screening path for large candidate pools and interactive shortlist work; it favors speed over exact-path fidelity.
  • `window=...` is available on `lcss_similarity`, `lcss_distance`, `edr_distance`, `erp_distance`, and `twed_distance` so you can keep the dynamic-programming path local when large warps are not plausible.
  • Recommended workflow: score all candidates with `mode="fast"` first, then rerun the shortlisted pairs with `mode="exact"` before you present the result as a final analog claim.
